const CONFIG = {
  // Cache settings
  CACHE_TTL_SEC: 3600,            // Cache expiration (1 hour for stable config data)
  CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT_KB: 500,       // Max cache size (500KB for config)

  // Payload size limits
  PAYLOAD_WARNING_KB: 5000,       // Warn if payload > 5MB
  PAYLOAD_MAX_KB: 10000,          // Hard limit ~10MB (GAS serialization limit)

  // Pagination
  DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE: 500,         // Default rows per page
  MAX_PAGE_SIZE: 1000,            // Maximum rows per page

  // Performance
  BATCH_SIZE: 400,                // Rows per batch operation
  MAX_BATCH_SIZE: 500,            // Hard cap for sync payloads (OOM / timeout guard)
  LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS: 30000,         // 30 seconds lock timeout
  SCRIPT_TIMEOUT_BUFFER_MS: 25000, // Leave 25s buffer before 6min limit

  // Data filtering
  DEFAULT_RECENT_DAYS: 30,        // Default days for recent data filter

  // Session security
  SESSION_INACTIVITY_MS: 30 * 60 * 1000  // 30-min inactivity auto-logout threshold
};

function doGet(e) {
  return HtmlService.createHtmlOutputFromFile('InsoilTool')
    .setTitle(`Insoil Tool ${APP_VERSION} (${BUILD_TIMESTAMP})`)
    .setXFrameOptionsMode(HtmlService.XFrameOptionsMode.ALLOWALL)
    .addMetaTag('viewport', 'width=device-width, initial-scale=1');
}

/**
 * Safe JSON.parse that never throws.
 * Returns { ok: true, data } on success or { ok: false, error } on failure.
 * Usage: const { ok, data, error } = safeJsonParse_(str, []);
 */
function safeJsonParse_(str, fallback) {
  if (str === null || str === undefined || str === '') {
    return { ok: true, data: fallback !== undefined ? fallback : null };
  }
  if (typeof str !== 'string') {
    // Already an object/array — return as-is
    return { ok: true, data: str };
  }
  try {
    return { ok: true, data: JSON.parse(str) };
  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, data: fallback !== undefined ? fallback : null, error: 'Invalid JSON: ' + e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Hash a plaintext password using SHA-256 (hex string).
 * Used for storing and comparing credentials — never store plaintext.
 */
function hashPassword_(plaintext) {
  if (!plaintext) return '';
  const bytes = Utilities.computeDigest(
    Utilities.DigestAlgorithm.SHA_256,
    String(plaintext),
    Utilities.Charset.UTF_8
  );
  return bytes.map(b => ('0' + (b & 0xff).toString(16)).slice(-2)).join('');
}

/**
 * Require the caller to have at least the specified role.
 * Role hierarchy: Admin > manager > operator > viewer
 * Throws if the caller cannot be identified or has insufficient role.
 */
function requireRole_(minRole) {
  const HIERARCHY = { admin: 4, manager: 3, operator: 2, viewer: 1 };
  const minLevel = HIERARCHY[(minRole || 'operator').toLowerCase()] || 2;

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sheet = ss.getSheetByName('Users');
  if (!sheet) throw new Error('Access denied: Users sheet not found.');

  const callerEmail = (Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || '').toLowerCase().trim();
  if (!callerEmail) {
    throw new Error('Access denied: unable to identify the caller. A signed-in Google account is required.');
  }

  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    const rowEmail = String(data[i][1] || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    const rowRole  = String(data[i][3] || '').toLowerCase().trim();
    if (rowEmail === callerEmail) {
      const callerLevel = HIERARCHY[rowRole] || 0;
      if (callerLevel >= minLevel) return callerEmail;
      throw new Error('Access denied: ' + callerEmail + ' requires role ' + minRole + ' or higher.');
    }
  }
  throw new Error('Access denied: ' + callerEmail + ' is not registered.');
}

/**
 * Append a data-mutation audit record for compliance/traceability.
 * action  — e.g. 'sync_to_qc', 'import_rgb', 'delete_rgb'
 * details — short string with counts or IDs changed
 */
function appendDataAudit_(action, sheetName, rowCount, details) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    let auditSheet = ss.getSheetByName('Admin_Audit_Log');
    if (!auditSheet) {
      auditSheet = ss.insertSheet('Admin_Audit_Log');
      auditSheet.appendRow(['Timestamp', 'Action', 'Actor', 'Target', 'Details']);
    }
    const actor = Session.getActiveUser().getEmail() || 'unknown';
    auditSheet.appendRow([
      new Date(), action, actor,
      sheetName || '', (details ? details + ' ' : '') + 'rows=' + (rowCount || 0)
    ]);
  } catch (e) {
    // Audit failures must never crash the primary operation
    console.error('appendDataAudit_ failed:', e.message);
  }
}

/**
 * =============================
 * MODULE 1: CONFIG & DATA LOAD
 * =============================
 */

function getDataByHeaders(ss, sheetName, headers) {
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sh) return [];

  const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return [];

  const headerRow = data[0].map(h => String(h).trim());
  const idxMap = headers.map(h => headerRow.indexOf(h));

  // If any header is missing, return empty to prevent offset errors
  if (idxMap.some(i => i === -1)) return [];

  const out = [];
  for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    const row = data[r];
    out.push(idxMap.map(c => {
      const v = row[c];
      // Inline Sanitization: Remove Excel Errors (#N/A) and Infinite numbers
      if (v instanceof Error) return "";
      if (typeof v === 'number' && !isFinite(v)) return "";
      return (v === null || v === undefined) ? "" : v;
    }));
  }
  return out;
}
/**
 * CORRECTED getInitialData
 * - Removes duplicate definitions
 * - Calls the correct helper: getDataByHeaders (plural, no underscore)
 */
// ============================================================================
// INITIAL LOAD (Frontend calls google.script.run.getInitialData())
// Must ALWAYS return an object (never null)
// ============================================================================

function getInitialData() {
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cacheKey = 'initialData:v1';
  const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(cached);
    } catch (err) {
      // Ignore cache parse errors and fall back to live read.
    }
  }
  const traceId = 'SRV-' + new Date().toISOString() + '-' + Utilities.getUuid();
  const t0 = Date.now();

  // IMPORTANT: Never return undefined. Return OK/ERROR envelope always.
  const out = {
    __traceId: traceId,
    __status: 'OK',
    __missingSheets: [],
    __info: { ms: 0, counts: {} },

    // payload keys used by frontend
    criteria: [],
    stv: [],
    qcCriteria: [],
    blanks: [],
    registry: [],
    serviceConfig: [],
    recipeConfig: {}
  };

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (!ss) {
      // If this is a standalone script (not bound), this can be null.
      // Return ERROR envelope (so frontend shows message), do not return undefined.
      out.__status = 'ERROR';
      out.__error = 'No active spreadsheet. If this is a standalone Apps Script, bind it to a Sheet or open by ID.';
      out.__info.ms = Date.now() - t0;
      console.log('[getInitialData] ERROR no active spreadsheet traceId=' + traceId);
      return out;
    }

    // Ensure Crop columns exist/default before initial load (RGB_Data + QC_Data + SoilTest_Results)
    const rgbSheet = ss.getSheetByName('RGB_Data');
    if (rgbSheet) {
      const rgbHeaderRow = findHeaderRowIndexByKeys_(rgbSheet, ['deviceid', 'testid']);
      if (rgbHeaderRow > 0) {
        ensureColumnWithDefault_(rgbSheet, rgbHeaderRow, 'Crop', 'Wheat');
        moveColumnAfter_(rgbSheet, rgbHeaderRow, 'Crop', 'Farm_ID');
      }
    }
    const qcSheet = ss.getSheetByName('QC_Data');
    if (qcSheet) {
      const qcHeaderRow = findHeaderRowIndexByKeys_(qcSheet, ['deviceid', 'testid']);
      if (qcHeaderRow > 0) {
        ensureColumnWithDefault_(qcSheet, qcHeaderRow, 'Crop', 'Wheat');
        moveColumnAfter_(qcSheet, qcHeaderRow, 'Crop', 'Farm_ID');
      }
    }
    ensureSoilTestResultsSheet_();
    const soilSheet = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    if (soilSheet) {
      ensureColumnWithDefault_(soilSheet, 1, 'Crop', 'Wheat');
      moveColumnAfter_(soilSheet, 1, 'Crop', 'Farm_ID');
    }

    // Inventory Manager sheets
    ensureInventorySheets_(ss, out.__missingSheets);

    out.criteria = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Criteria', out.__missingSheets);
    out.stv = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'STV', out.__missingSheets);
    out.qcCriteria = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'QC_Criteria', out.__missingSheets);
    out.blanks = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Blanks', out.__missingSheets);
    if (!out.blanks.length) {
      out.blanks = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Blank_Values', out.__missingSheets);
    }
    if (!out.blanks.length) {
      out.blanks = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Blank Values', out.__missingSheets);
    }
    if (!out.blanks.length) {
      out.blanks = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'BlankValues', out.__missingSheets);
    }
    out.registry = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Device_Registry', out.__missingSheets);
    out.serviceConfig = readSheetAsObjects(ss, 'Service_Config', out.__missingSheets);
    out.stvInfo = getLatestStvInfo_(ss);

    // Optional key-value style sheet (safe if missing)
    out.recipeConfig = readKeyValueSheet(ss, 'Recipe_Config', out.__missingSheets);

    // counts
    out.__info.counts = {
      criteria: out.criteria.length,
      stv: out.stv.length,
      qcCriteria: out.qcCriteria.length,
      blanks: out.blanks.length,
      registry: out.registry.length,
      serviceConfig: out.serviceConfig.length
    };

    out.__info.ms = Date.now() - t0;
    console.log('[getInitialData] OK traceId=' + traceId + ' ms=' + out.__info.ms +
      ' missing=' + JSON.stringify(out.__missingSheets) +
      ' counts=' + JSON.stringify(out.__info.counts));
    
    // Safe caching: only cache if payload is reasonably sized
    try {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(out);
      const sizeKB = Math.round(serialized.length / 1024);
      out.__info.payloadSizeKB = sizeKB;
      
      // GAS cache limit is ~100KB per entry; serialization limit is ~10MB
      // Only cache config data if < CONFIG.CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT_KB (conservative)
      if (sizeKB < CONFIG.CACHE_SIZE_LIMIT_KB) {
        cache.put(cacheKey, serialized, CONFIG.CACHE_TTL_SEC);
        console.log('[getInitialData] Cached payload sizeKB=' + sizeKB + ' traceId=' + traceId);
      } else {
        console.warn('[getInitialData] Payload too large for cache: ' + sizeKB + 'KB traceId=' + traceId);
      }
    } catch (cacheErr) {
      console.error('[getInitialData] Cache failed: ' + (cacheErr.message || cacheErr) + ' traceId=' + traceId);
    }
    
    return out;

  } catch (err) {
    out.__status = 'ERROR';
    out.__error = (err && err.message) ? err.message : String(err);
    out.__stack = (err && err.stack) ? String(err.stack) : '';
    out.__info.ms = Date.now() - t0;

    console.log('[getInitialData] ERROR traceId=' + traceId + ' ms=' + out.__info.ms + ' err=' + out.__error);
    return out; // never undefined
  }
}

// =============================
// DASHBOARD SUMMARY
// =============================
function getDashboardData() {
  const out = {
    status: 'OK',
    generatedAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    metrics: {
      devices: 0,
      rgbRecords: 0,
      qaqcRecords: 0,
      soilResults: 0,
      uniqueFarms: 0
    },
    sources: [],
    missingSheets: []
  };

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) {
    out.status = 'ERROR';
    out.error = 'No active spreadsheet found.';
    return out;
  }

  const metricSheets = [
    { key: 'devices', name: 'Device_Registry' },
    { key: 'rgbRecords', name: 'RGB_Data' },
    { key: 'qaqcRecords', name: 'QC_Data' },
    { key: 'soilResults', name: 'SoilTest_Results' }
  ];

  metricSheets.forEach(entry => {
    const count = getSheetRowCount_(ss, entry.name, out.missingSheets);
    if (typeof count === 'number') {
      out.metrics[entry.key] = count;
      out.sources.push({ name: entry.name, rows: count });
    }
  });

  out.metrics.uniqueFarms = getUniqueFarmCount_(ss, out.missingSheets);

  return out;
}

function getSheetRowCount_(ss, sheetName, missingSheetsArr) {
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sh) {
    addMissingSheet_(missingSheetsArr, sheetName);
    return 0;
  }
  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  return Math.max(0, lastRow - 1);
}

function getUniqueFarmCount_(ss, missingSheetsArr) {
  const farmSheets = ['SoilTest_Results', 'RGB_Data', 'QC_Data'];
  for (let i = 0; i < farmSheets.length; i++) {
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(farmSheets[i]);
    if (!sh) {
      addMissingSheet_(missingSheetsArr, farmSheets[i]);
      continue;
    }
    const headerRow = Math.max(1, findHeaderRowIndexByKeys_(sh, ['farm']));
    const count = countUniqueValuesInSheet_(sh, headerRow, ['farm_id', 'farm id', 'farmid']);
    if (count !== null) return count;
  }
  return 0;
}

function countUniqueValuesInSheet_(sheet, headerRow, headerCandidates) {
  if (!sheet) return null;
  const lastRow = sheet.getLastRow();
  const lastCol = sheet.getLastColumn();
  if (lastRow <= headerRow || lastCol < 1) return 0;

  const headers = sheet.getRange(headerRow, 1, 1, lastCol).getDisplayValues()[0];
  const targetCol = findHeaderIndex_(headers, headerCandidates);
  if (targetCol < 0) return null;

  const values = sheet.getRange(headerRow + 1, targetCol + 1, lastRow - headerRow, 1).getDisplayValues();
  const unique = new Set();
  values.forEach(row => {
    const v = String(row[0] || '').trim();
    if (v) unique.add(v);
  });
  return unique.size;
}

function findHeaderIndex_(headers, candidates) {
  const normalizedCandidates = (candidates || []).map(c => normalizeHeader_(c));
  for (let i = 0; i < headers.length; i++) {
    const normalized = normalizeHeader_(headers[i]);
    if (!normalized) continue;
    if (normalizedCandidates.includes(normalized)) return i;
  }
  return -1;
}

function normalizeHeader_(value) {
  return String(value || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[\s_.-]/g, '');
}

function addMissingSheet_(missingSheetsArr, sheetName) {
  if (!Array.isArray(missingSheetsArr)) return;
  if (!sheetName) return;
  if (!missingSheetsArr.includes(sheetName)) missingSheetsArr.push(sheetName);
}

// =============================
// STV DIRECT (CSV IMPORT + READ)
// =============================
const STV_DIRECT_SHEET = 'STV_Direct';
const STV_DIRECT_HEADERS = [
  'ClientID',
  'Test_ID',
  'Device_ID',
  'Farmer',
  'Farm_ID',
  'Crop',
  'Date',
  'Test Date-time',
  'pH',
  'EC',
  'OC',
  'Avail_N',
  'Avail_P',
  'Avail_K',
  'Avail_S',
  'Zn',
  'Fe',
  'Mn',
  'Cu',
  'B'
];

function stvDirectLog_(message, data) {
  try {
    if (data !== undefined) {
      Logger.log('[STV_DIRECT] ' + message + ' ' + JSON.stringify(data));
      return;
    }
    Logger.log('[STV_DIRECT] ' + message);
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('[STV_DIRECT] log failed: ' + err);
  }
}

let stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = null;

function stvDirectLogNutrientSourcesTable_(clientId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (!ss) {
      stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources table: no active spreadsheet');
      stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = { error: 'No active spreadsheet' };
      return stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_;
    }
    const sheetName = 'NutrientSources';
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
    if (!sh) {
      stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources table: sheet missing', { sheet: sheetName });
      stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = { error: 'Sheet missing', sheet: sheetName };
      return stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_;
    }
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
      stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources table: empty sheet', { sheet: sheetName });
      stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = { error: 'Empty sheet', sheet: sheetName };
      return stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_;
    }
    const headers = data[0].map(h => String(h || '').trim());
    const idx = {
      ClientID: headers.indexOf('ClientID'),
      SourceID: headers.indexOf('SourceID'),
      SourceName: headers.indexOf('SourceName'),
      Type: headers.indexOf('Type'),
      GradeN: headers.indexOf('GradeN'),
      GradeP2O5: headers.indexOf('GradeP2O5'),
      GradeK2O: headers.indexOf('GradeK2O'),
      GradeS: headers.indexOf('GradeS'),
      GradeZn: headers.indexOf('GradeZn'),
      GradeFe: headers.indexOf('GradeFe'),
      GradeCu: headers.indexOf('GradeCu'),
      GradeMn: headers.indexOf('GradeMn'),
      GradeB: headers.indexOf('GradeB'),
      SuitableMethods: headers.indexOf('SuitableMethods'),
      DefaultUseStage: headers.indexOf('DefaultUseStage')
    };
    stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources headers', { headers: headers, indices: idx });

    const cid = String(clientId || 'Default').trim();
    const clientRows = data.slice(1).filter(row => {
      const rowClient = String(row[idx.ClientID] || '').trim();
      return rowClient === cid || rowClient === 'Default';
    });
    const sampleRows = clientRows.slice(0, 5).map(row => ({
      ClientID: row[idx.ClientID],
      SourceID: row[idx.SourceID],
      SourceName: row[idx.SourceName],
      Type: row[idx.Type],
      GradeZn: row[idx.GradeZn],
      GradeCu: row[idx.GradeCu],
      GradeMn: row[idx.GradeMn],
      GradeFe: row[idx.GradeFe],
      GradeB: row[idx.GradeB],
      SuitableMethods: row[idx.SuitableMethods],
      DefaultUseStage: row[idx.DefaultUseStage]
    }));
    const microRows = clientRows.filter(row => {
      const gradeZn = Number(row[idx.GradeZn] || 0);
      const gradeFe = Number(row[idx.GradeFe] || 0);
      const gradeCu = Number(row[idx.GradeCu] || 0);
      const gradeMn = Number(row[idx.GradeMn] || 0);
      const gradeB = Number(row[idx.GradeB] || 0);
      return gradeZn > 0 || gradeFe > 0 || gradeCu > 0 || gradeMn > 0 || gradeB > 0;
    }).slice(0, 10).map(row => ({
      ClientID: row[idx.ClientID],
      SourceID: row[idx.SourceID],
      SourceName: row[idx.SourceName],
      Type: row[idx.Type],
      GradeZn: row[idx.GradeZn],
      GradeFe: row[idx.GradeFe],
      GradeCu: row[idx.GradeCu],
      GradeMn: row[idx.GradeMn],
      GradeB: row[idx.GradeB],
      SuitableMethods: row[idx.SuitableMethods],
      DefaultUseStage: row[idx.DefaultUseStage]
    }));
    stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources sample rows', { clientId: cid, rows: sampleRows });
    stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources micronutrient rows', { clientId: cid, count: microRows.length, rows: microRows });
    stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = {
      clientId: cid,
      headers: headers,
      indices: idx,
      sampleRows: sampleRows,
      micronutrientRows: microRows,
      clientRowCount: clientRows.length
    };
    return stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_;
  } catch (err) {
    const payload = { error: String(err) };
    stvDirectLog_('NutrientSources table logger failed', payload);
    stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_ = payload;
    return stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_;
  }
}

function getSTVDirectData() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) {
    return { status: 'ERROR', error: 'No active spreadsheet found.' };
  }
  ensureSTVDirectSheet_(ss);
  stvDirectLog_('getSTVDirectData reading sheet', { sheet: STV_DIRECT_SHEET });
  const rows = readSheetAsObjects(ss, STV_DIRECT_SHEET);
  stvDirectLog_('getSTVDirectData rows loaded', { count: rows.length });
  return { status: 'OK', rows: rows };
}

function srvr_ImportSTVDirectCSV(payload) {
  const csvText = payload && payload.csvText ? String(payload.csvText) : '';
  if (!csvText.trim()) {
    return { addedCount: 0, errorCount: 1, errors: ['CSV is empty.'] };
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) {
    return { addedCount: 0, errorCount: 1, errors: ['No active spreadsheet found.'] };
  }

  const sheet = ensureSTVDirectSheet_(ss);
  const parsed = Utilities.parseCsv(csvText);
  if (!parsed.length) {
    return { addedCount: 0, errorCount: 1, errors: ['CSV could not be parsed.'] };
  }

  const headerRow = parsed[0] || [];
  const headerMap = buildHeaderIndexMap_(headerRow);
  const requiredHeaders = [
    'Test_ID',
    'Device_ID',
    'Farm_ID',
    'Crop',
    'pH',
    'EC',
    'OC',
    'Avail_N',
    'Avail_P',
    'Avail_K',
    'Avail_S',
    'Zn',
    'Fe',
    'Mn',
    'Cu',
    'B'
  ];
  const missing = requiredHeaders.filter(h => headerMap[normalizeHeader_(h)] == null);
  const hasTestDate = headerMap[normalizeHeader_('Test Date-time')] != null
    || headerMap[normalizeHeader_('Date')] != null;
  if (!hasTestDate) {
    missing.push('Date/Test Date-time');
  }
  if (missing.length) {
    return { addedCount: 0, errorCount: missing.length, errors: [`Missing headers: ${missing.join(', ')}`] };
  }

  const rows = [];
  for (let i = 1; i < parsed.length; i++) {
    const row = parsed[i];
    if (!row || row.join('').trim() === '') continue;
    const out = STV_DIRECT_HEADERS.map(h => {
      const key = normalizeHeader_(h);
      if (key === normalizeHeader_('ClientID')) {
        const idx = headerMap[normalizeHeader_('ClientID')];
        return idx != null ? row[idx] : 'Default';
      }
      if (key === normalizeHeader_('Farmer')) {
        const idx = headerMap[normalizeHeader_('Farmer')] ?? headerMap[normalizeHeader_('ClientID')];
        return idx != null ? row[idx] : '';
      }
      if (key === normalizeHeader_('Test Date-time')) {
        const idx = headerMap[normalizeHeader_('Test Date-time')] ?? headerMap[normalizeHeader_('Date')];
        return idx != null ? row[idx] : '';
      }
      const idx = headerMap[key];
      return idx != null ? row[idx] : '';
    });
    rows.push(out);
  }

  if (!rows.length) {
    return { addedCount: 0, errorCount: 0 };
  }

  sheet.getRange(sheet.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rows.length, STV_DIRECT_HEADERS.length).setValues(rows);
  return { addedCount: rows.length, errorCount: 0 };
}

function ensureSTVDirectSheet_(ss) {
  let sheet = ss.getSheetByName(STV_DIRECT_SHEET);
  if (!sheet) {
    sheet = ss.insertSheet(STV_DIRECT_SHEET);
  }
  const headerRange = sheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, STV_DIRECT_HEADERS.length);
  const existing = headerRange.getValues()[0];
  const hasHeader = existing.some(cell => String(cell || '').trim() !== '');
  if (!hasHeader) {
    headerRange.setValues([STV_DIRECT_HEADERS]);
  }
  return sheet;
}

function buildHeaderIndexMap_(headerRow) {
  const map = {};
  headerRow.forEach((h, idx) => {
    const key = normalizeHeader_(h);
    if (key && map[key] == null) map[key] = idx;
  });
  return map;
}

function srvrComputeStvDirectRdfNpk(payload) {
  try {
    const input = payload || {};
    const clientId = input.clientId || 'Default';
    const cropId = input.cropId || 'Default';
    const ratingSchemeId = input.ratingSchemeId || 'LMH';
    const methodId = stvDirectNormalizeMethodId_(input.methodId || 'SOIL');
    const stageId = input.stageId || 'GENERAL';
    const targetYield = Number(input.targetYield) || 0;
    const effectiveClientId = stvDirectResolveClientId_(clientId, cropId);
    stvDirectLog_('srvrComputeStvDirectRdfNpk input', {
      clientId,
      cropId,
      ratingSchemeId,
      methodId,
      stageId,
      targetYield,
      effectiveClientId
    });

    const soilValues = input.soilValues || {};
    const soilValuesByParam = {
      N: Number(soilValues.N),
      P2O5: Number(soilValues.P),
      K2O: Number(soilValues.K),
      S: Number(soilValues.S),
      Cu: Number(soilValues.Cu),
      Zn: Number(soilValues.Zn),
      Fe: Number(soilValues.Fe),
      Mn: Number(soilValues.Mn),
      B: Number(soilValues.B)
    };
    stvDirectLog_('srvrComputeStvDirectRdfNpk soilValuesByParam', soilValuesByParam);

    const ratingsByParam = buildRatingsByParam_(clientId, ratingSchemeId, soilValuesByParam);
    const nutrients = {};
    ['N', 'P2O5', 'K2O'].forEach(nutrient => {
      const soilValue = soilValuesByParam[nutrient];
      if (!Number.isFinite(soilValue)) return;
      const ratingInfo = ratingsByParam[nutrient] || { rating: '', multiplier: 0 };
      const doseInfo = computeDoseFromRDF_(
        clientId,
        cropId,
        stageId,
        methodId,
        ratingSchemeId,
        nutrient,
        ratingInfo,
        targetYield
      );
      if (!doseInfo) return;
      nutrients[nutrient] = {
        soilValue: soilValue,
        rating: ratingInfo.rating || '',
        multiplier: ratingInfo.multiplier || 0,
        dose_kg_ha: doseInfo.dose_kg_ha,
        rdf_kg_ha: doseInfo.rdf_kg_ha || null,
        dosePerTonneYield: doseInfo.dosePerTonneYield || null,
        minDosePerHa: doseInfo.minDosePerHa || null,
        maxDosePerHa: doseInfo.maxDosePerHa || null,
        notes: doseInfo.notes || ''
      };
    });

    return {
      status: 'OK',
      data: {
        clientId: clientId,
        cropId: cropId,
        ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
        methodId: methodId,
        stageId: stageId,
        targetYield: targetYield,
        nutrients: nutrients
      }
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      error: (err && err.message) ? err.message : String(err)
    };
  }
}

function stvDirectBuildRatingsByParam_(clientID, ratingSchemeID, soilValuesByParam) {
  stvDirectLog_('stvDirectBuildRatingsByParam_ load ratings', {
    clientID,
    ratingSchemeID,
    params: Object.keys(soilValuesByParam || {})
  });
  const ratings = buildRatingsByParam_(clientID, ratingSchemeID, soilValuesByParam);
  if ((!ratings || Object.keys(ratings).length === 0) && clientID && clientID !== 'Default') {
    Logger.log('stvDirectBuildRatingsByParam fallback to Default for clientId=' + clientID);
    return buildRatingsByParam_('Default', ratingSchemeID, soilValuesByParam);
  }
  return ratings;
}

function stvDirectNormalizeMethodId_(methodID) {
  if (!methodID) return 'SOIL';
  if (methodID === 'RDF') return 'SOIL';
  return methodID;
}

function stvDirectHasNutrientMasterRows_(clientID, cropID) {
  try {
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientMasterRows_ checking sheet', {
      sheet: 'NutrientMaster',
      clientID,
      cropID
    });
    const master = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientMaster');
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientMasterRows_ rows loaded', { count: master?.rows?.length || 0 });
    const cClient = master.getCol('ClientID');
    const cCrop = master.getCol('CropID');
    return master.rows.some(row =>
      String(row[cClient]).trim() === String(clientID).trim() &&
      String(row[cCrop]).trim() === String(cropID).trim()
    );
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('stvDirectHasNutrientMasterRows_ error: ' + err);
    return false;
  }
}

function stvDirectHasNutrientStagesRows_(clientID, cropID) {
  try {
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientStagesRows_ checking sheet', {
      sheet: 'NutrientStages',
      clientID,
      cropID
    });
    const stages = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientStages');
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientStagesRows_ rows loaded', { count: stages?.rows?.length || 0 });
    const cClient = stages.getCol('ClientID');
    const cCrop = stages.getCol('CropID');
    return stages.rows.some(row =>
      String(row[cClient]).trim() === String(clientID).trim() &&
      String(row[cCrop]).trim() === String(cropID).trim()
    );
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('stvDirectHasNutrientStagesRows_ error: ' + err);
    return false;
  }
}

function stvDirectHasNutrientSourcesRows_(clientID) {
  try {
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientSourcesRows_ checking sheet', {
      sheet: 'NutrientSources',
      clientID
    });
    const sources = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientSources');
    stvDirectLog_('stvDirectHasNutrientSourcesRows_ rows loaded', { count: sources?.rows?.length || 0 });
    const cClient = sources.getCol('ClientID');
    return sources.rows.some(row => String(row[cClient]).trim() === String(clientID).trim());
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('stvDirectHasNutrientSourcesRows_ error: ' + err);
    return false;
  }
}

function stvDirectResolveClientId_(clientID, cropID) {
  const trimmedClient = String(clientID || '').trim();
  if (!trimmedClient || trimmedClient === 'Default') return 'Default';
  if (stvDirectHasNutrientMasterRows_(trimmedClient, cropID) ||
    stvDirectHasNutrientStagesRows_(trimmedClient, cropID) ||
    stvDirectHasNutrientSourcesRows_(trimmedClient)) {
    return trimmedClient;
  }
  Logger.log('stvDirectResolveClientId fallback to Default for clientId=%s cropId=%s', trimmedClient, cropID);
  return 'Default';
}

function stvDirectResolveStageId_(clientID, cropID, stageID) {
  if (stageID && stageID !== 'GENERAL') return stageID;
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) return stageID || 'GENERAL';
  const stData = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientStages');
  if (!stData || !stData.headers || !stData.rows) return stageID || 'GENERAL';
  const headers = stData.headers || [];
  const clientKey = 'ClientID';
  const cropKey = 'CropID';
  const stageKey = 'StageID';
  const seqKey = 'SequenceNo';
  const idxClient = headers.indexOf(clientKey);
  const idxCrop = headers.indexOf(cropKey);
  const idxStage = headers.indexOf(stageKey);
  const idxSeq = headers.indexOf(seqKey);
  if (idxClient < 0 || idxCrop < 0 || idxStage < 0) {
    Logger.log('stvDirectResolveStageId missing headers: client=%s crop=%s stage=%s',
      idxClient, idxCrop, idxStage);
    return stageID || 'GENERAL';
  }
  const rows = stData.rows.filter(row => row && row[idxStage]);
  const normalize = value => String(value || '').trim();
  const cropMatch = normalize(cropID);
  const clientMatch = normalize(clientID);
  const defaultMatch = 'Default';
  const candidates = rows
    .filter(row => normalize(row[idxCrop]) === cropMatch)
    .filter(row => {
      const rowClient = normalize(row[idxClient]);
      return rowClient === clientMatch || rowClient === defaultMatch;
    })
    .map(row => ({
      stageId: row[idxStage],
      seq: Number(row[idxSeq]) || 0,
      clientId: normalize(row[idxClient])
    }))
    .sort((a, b) => (a.seq - b.seq) || (a.clientId === defaultMatch ? 1 : -1));
  if (candidates.length) {
    const resolved = candidates[0].stageId;
    Logger.log('stvDirectResolveStageId using %s for cropId=%s clientId=%s', resolved, cropID, clientID);
    return resolved;
  }
  Logger.log('stvDirectResolveStageId fallback stageId=%s cropId=%s clientId=%s', stageID, cropID, clientID);
  return stageID || 'GENERAL';
}

function stvDirectComputeDoseFromRdf_(clientID, cropID, stageID, methodID, ratingSchemeID, nutrient, ratingInfo, targetYield) {
  const normalizedMethodId = stvDirectNormalizeMethodId_(methodID);
  let result = computeDoseFromRDF_(clientID, cropID, stageID, normalizedMethodId, ratingSchemeID, nutrient, ratingInfo, targetYield);
  if (!result && clientID && clientID !== 'Default') {
    Logger.log('stvDirectComputeDoseFromRdf fallback to Default clientId for nutrient=' + nutrient);
    result = computeDoseFromRDF_('Default', cropID, stageID, normalizedMethodId, ratingSchemeID, nutrient, ratingInfo, targetYield);
  }
  Logger.log('stvDirectComputeDoseFromRdf nutrient=%s cropId=%s stageId=%s methodId=%s ratingSchemeId=%s rating=%s multiplier=%s result=%s',
    nutrient, cropID, stageID, normalizedMethodId, ratingSchemeID,
    ratingInfo && ratingInfo.rating ? ratingInfo.rating : '',
    ratingInfo && ratingInfo.multiplier != null ? ratingInfo.multiplier : '',
    result ? JSON.stringify(result) : 'null');
  return result;
}

function stvDirectBuildGeneralStages_(summary, cropId, clientId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield) {
  stvDirectLog_('stvDirectBuildGeneralStages_ start', {
    cropId,
    clientId,
    methodId,
    ratingSchemeId,
    targetYield,
    summaryCount: summary?.length || 0
  });
  return buildGeneralStages_(summary, cropId, clientId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield);
}

function stvDirectGetStageNames_(clientID, cropID) {
  try {
    const stData = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientStages');
    if (!stData || !stData.headers || !stData.rows) return ['Basal'];
    const headers = stData.headers || [];
    const idxClient = headers.indexOf('ClientID');
    const idxCrop = headers.indexOf('CropID');
    const idxStage = headers.indexOf('StageName') >= 0 ? headers.indexOf('StageName') : headers.indexOf('StageID');
    const idxSeq = headers.indexOf('SequenceNo');
    if (idxClient < 0 || idxCrop < 0 || idxStage < 0) return ['Basal'];
    const rows = stData.rows
      .filter(row => String(row[idxClient]).trim() === String(clientID).trim())
      .filter(row => String(row[idxCrop]).trim() === String(cropID).trim())
      .map(row => ({
        name: String(row[idxStage] || '').trim(),
        seq: Number(row[idxSeq]) || 0
      }))
      .filter(row => row.name);
    if (!rows.length && clientID !== 'Default') {
      return stvDirectGetStageNames_('Default', cropID);
    }
    rows.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
    return rows.length ? rows.map(row => row.name) : ['Basal'];
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('stvDirectGetStageNames_ error: ' + err);
    return ['Basal'];
  }
}

function stvDirectBuildMicronutrientRows_(nutrientRequirements, cropId, clientId) {
  const microKeys = ['CU', 'ZN', 'FE', 'MN', 'B'];
  const gradeKeyMap = {
    CU: 'Cu',
    ZN: 'Zn',
    FE: 'Fe',
    MN: 'Mn',
    B: 'B'
  };
  const sources = getNutrientSources_(clientId || 'Default');
  const stages = stvDirectGetStageNames_(clientId, cropId);
  const stageName = stages[0] || 'Basal';
  const rows = [];

  nutrientRequirements.forEach(req => {
    const nutrient = String(req.nutrient || '').trim();
    const key = nutrient.toUpperCase();
    if (!microKeys.includes(key)) return;
    const dose = Number(req.dose_kg_ha) || 0;
    if (dose <= 0) return;
    const gradeKey = gradeKeyMap[key] || key;
    const source = sources.find(src => Number(src[gradeKey]) > 0);
    if (!source) {
      Logger.log('stvDirectBuildMicronutrientRows_ no source for nutrient=' + nutrient);
      return;
    }
    const grade = Number(source[gradeKey]) || 0;
    if (!grade) return;
    const kgProductPerHa = dose / (grade / 100);
    rows.push({
      stage: stageName,
      nutrient: nutrient,
      timing: '',
      method: '',
      dose: '',
      unit: '',
      sourceMix: source.name,
      productQty: '',
      productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHa * 10) / 10,
      costPerHa: '',
      details: 'FERT: Micronutrient source',
      flags: ''
    });
  });

  Logger.log('stvDirectBuildMicronutrientRows_ rows=' + rows.length);
  return rows;
}

function stvDirectBuildFallbackSchedule_(nutrientRequirements, cropId, clientId) {
  const rows = [];
  const sources = getNutrientSources_(clientId || 'Default');
  const stages = stvDirectGetStageNames_(clientId, cropId);
  const stageName = stages[0] || 'Basal';

  function pickSource(predicate) {
    return sources.find(predicate) || null;
  }

  function pushRow(nutrientKey, source, doseKgHa) {
    if (!source) return;
    const grade = Number(source[nutrientKey]) || 0;
    if (!grade) return;
    const kgProductPerHa = doseKgHa / (grade / 100);
    rows.push({
      stage: stageName,
      nutrient: nutrientKey,
      timing: '',
      method: '',
      dose: '',
      unit: '',
      sourceMix: source.name,
      productQty: '',
      productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHa * 10) / 10,
      costPerHa: '',
      details: 'FERT: fallback schedule',
      flags: ''
    });
  }

  nutrientRequirements.forEach(req => {
    const nutrient = String(req.nutrient || '').trim();
    const dose = Number(req.dose_kg_ha) || 0;
    if (dose <= 0) return;
    if (nutrient === 'N') {
      const source = pickSource(src => src.N > 0 && src.type === 'Straight') ||
        pickSource(src => src.N > 0);
      pushRow('N', source, dose);
      return;
    }
    if (nutrient === 'P2O5') {
      const source = pickSource(src => src.P2O5 > 0 && src.type === 'Complex') ||
        pickSource(src => src.P2O5 > 0);
      pushRow('P2O5', source, dose);
      return;
    }
    if (nutrient === 'K2O') {
      const source = pickSource(src => src.K2O > 0 && src.type === 'Straight') ||
        pickSource(src => src.K2O > 0);
      pushRow('K2O', source, dose);
      return;
    }
    if (nutrient === 'S') {
      const source = pickSource(src => src.S > 0 && src.type === 'Straight') ||
        pickSource(src => src.S > 0);
      pushRow('S', source, dose);
      return;
    }
  });

  Logger.log('stvDirectBuildFallbackSchedule_ rows=' + rows.length);
  return rows;
}

function stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements_(input) {
  const {
    clientId,
    cropId,
    ratingSchemeId,
    methodId,
    stageId,
    targetYield,
    soilValuesByParam
  } = input;

  const normalizedMethodId = stvDirectNormalizeMethodId_(methodId);
  const resolvedStageId = stvDirectResolveStageId_(clientId, cropId, stageId);
  const ratingsByParam = stvDirectBuildRatingsByParam_(clientId, ratingSchemeId, soilValuesByParam);
  const requirements = [];
  Logger.log('stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements input: ' + JSON.stringify({
    clientId: clientId,
    cropId: cropId,
    ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
    methodId: normalizedMethodId,
    stageId: resolvedStageId,
    targetYield: targetYield,
    soilValuesByParam: soilValuesByParam,
    ratingsByParam: ratingsByParam
  }));

  ['N', 'P2O5', 'K2O', 'S', 'Cu', 'Zn', 'Fe', 'Mn', 'B'].forEach(nutrient => {
    const soilValue = soilValuesByParam[nutrient];
    if (!Number.isFinite(soilValue)) {
      Logger.log('stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements skip nutrient=' + nutrient + ' soilValue=' + soilValue);
      return;
    }
    const ratingInfo = ratingsByParam[nutrient] || { rating: '', multiplier: 0 };
    Logger.log('stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements nutrient=' + nutrient +
      ' soilValue=' + soilValue +
      ' rating=' + (ratingInfo.rating || '') +
      ' multiplier=' + (ratingInfo.multiplier || 0));
    const doseInfo = stvDirectComputeDoseFromRdf_(
      clientId,
      cropId,
      resolvedStageId,
      normalizedMethodId,
      ratingSchemeId,
      nutrient,
      ratingInfo,
      targetYield
    );
    if (!doseInfo) {
      Logger.log('stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements missing doseInfo for nutrient=' + nutrient);
      return;
    }
    requirements.push({
      nutrient: nutrient,
      soilValue: soilValue,
      rating: ratingInfo.rating || '',
      multiplier: ratingInfo.multiplier || 0,
      dose_kg_ha: doseInfo.dose_kg_ha,
      rdf_kg_ha: doseInfo.rdf_kg_ha || null,
      dosePerTonneYield: doseInfo.dosePerTonneYield || null,
      minDosePerHa: doseInfo.minDosePerHa || null,
      maxDosePerHa: doseInfo.maxDosePerHa || null,
      notes: doseInfo.notes || ''
    });
  });

  return requirements;
}

function srvrComputeStvDirectDosePlan(payload) {
  try {
    const input = payload || {};
    const clientId = input.clientId || 'Default';
    const cropId = input.cropId || 'Default';
    const ratingSchemeId = input.ratingSchemeId || 'LMH';
    const methodId = stvDirectNormalizeMethodId_(input.methodId || 'SOIL');
    const stageId = input.stageId || 'GENERAL';
    const targetYield = Number(input.targetYield) || 0;
    const effectiveClientId = stvDirectResolveClientId_(clientId, cropId);

    const soilValues = input.soilValues || {};
    const soilValuesByParam = {
      N: Number(soilValues.N),
      P2O5: Number(soilValues.P),
      K2O: Number(soilValues.K),
      S: Number(soilValues.S),
      Cu: Number(soilValues.Cu),
      Zn: Number(soilValues.Zn),
      Fe: Number(soilValues.Fe),
      Mn: Number(soilValues.Mn),
      B: Number(soilValues.B)
    };
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan input: clientId=%s cropId=%s ratingSchemeId=%s methodId=%s stageId=%s targetYield=%s',
      clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, stageId, targetYield);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan effectiveClientId=%s', effectiveClientId);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan soilValuesByParam: ' + JSON.stringify(soilValuesByParam));

    const nutrientSources = getNutrientSources_(effectiveClientId);
    const nutrientRequirements = stvDirectComputeNutrientRequirements_({
      clientId: effectiveClientId,
      cropId: cropId,
      ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
      methodId: methodId,
      stageId: stageId,
      targetYield: targetYield,
      soilValuesByParam: soilValuesByParam
    });

    const summaryRows = nutrientRequirements.map(req => {
      const nutrientKey = String(req.nutrient || '').trim();
      const unit = ['N', 'P2O5', 'K2O'].includes(nutrientKey) ? 'kg/ha' : 'mg/kg';
      return {
        nutrient: nutrientKey,
        soilValue: req.soilValue,
        unit: unit,
        className: req.rating || '',
        notes: req.notes || '',
        baseDose: req.dose_kg_ha || 0,
        doseMultiplier: 1
      };
    });
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan summaryRows.length=' + summaryRows.length);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan summaryRows nutrients=' + summaryRows.map(row => row.nutrient).join(','));

    const fertilizerDoses = stvDirectBuildGeneralStages_(
      summaryRows,
      cropId,
      effectiveClientId,
      methodId,
      ratingSchemeId,
      targetYield
    );
    const stageOrder = stvDirectGetStageNames_(effectiveClientId, cropId);
    const filteredFertilizerDoses = fertilizerDoses.filter(row => {
      if (!row) return false;

      const mix = String(row.sourceMix || '').trim();
      if (!mix) return false;

      // Prefer numeric kg/ha field; if missing, treat as 0 (drop)
      const kgHa = Number(
        row.productKgPerHa ??
        row.productQtyKgHa ??
        row.doseKgHa ??
        row.dose ??
        0
      );

      return Number.isFinite(kgHa) && kgHa > 0;
    });

    const microDoses = stvDirectBuildMicronutrientRows_(nutrientRequirements, cropId, effectiveClientId);
    let combinedDoses = filteredFertilizerDoses.concat(microDoses);
    if (combinedDoses.length === 0 && nutrientRequirements.length > 0) {
      const fallbackDoses = stvDirectBuildFallbackSchedule_(nutrientRequirements, cropId, effectiveClientId);
      combinedDoses = combinedDoses.concat(fallbackDoses);
    }
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan nutrientRequirements.length=' + nutrientRequirements.length);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan nutrientSources.length=' + nutrientSources.length);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan fertilizerDoses.length=' + fertilizerDoses.length);
    Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan microDoses.length=' + microDoses.length);
    if (fertilizerDoses.length === 0) {
      Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan empty fertilizerDoses. nutrientRequirements=' + JSON.stringify(nutrientRequirements));
      Logger.log('stvDirectDosePlan empty fertilizerDoses. summaryRows=' + JSON.stringify(summaryRows));
    }

    return {
      status: 'OK',
      data: {
        clientId: effectiveClientId,
        cropId: cropId,
        ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
        methodId: methodId,
        stageId: stageId,
        targetYield: targetYield,
        nutrientRequirements: nutrientRequirements,
        summaryRows: summaryRows,
        fertilizerDoses: combinedDoses,
        stageOrder: stageOrder,
        nutrientSources: nutrientSources,
        nutrientSourcesDebug: stvDirectLastNutrientSourcesDebug_
      }
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return {
      status: 'ERROR',
      error: (err && err.message) ? err.message : String(err)
    };
  }
}

// =============================
// MODULE 2: SHEET READ HELPERS
// =============================
function readSheetAsObjects(ss, sheetName, missingSheetsArr) {
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sh) {
    if (Array.isArray(missingSheetsArr)) missingSheetsArr.push(sheetName);
    return [];
  }

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const lastCol = sh.getLastColumn();
  if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 1) return [];

  const headers = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lastCol).getValues()[0].map(h => String(h || '').trim());
  const data = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, lastCol).getValues();

  const rows = [];
  for (let r = 0; r < data.length; r++) {
    const obj = {};
    for (let c = 0; c < headers.length; c++) {
      const key = headers[c];
      if (!key) continue;

      let v = data[r][c];

      // Make Dates safely serializable (optional but helps prevent odd client-side behavior)
      if (v instanceof Date) v = v.toISOString();

      obj[key] = v;
    }
    rows.push(obj);
  }
  return rows;
}

function readKeyValueSheet(ss, sheetName, missingSheetsArr) {
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sh) {
    if (Array.isArray(missingSheetsArr)) missingSheetsArr.push(sheetName);
    return {};
  }

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  const lastCol = sh.getLastColumn();
  if (lastRow < 2 || lastCol < 2) return {};

  // Expect columns: Key | Value (anything beyond is ignored)
  const data = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, 2).getValues();
  const out = {};
  data.forEach(row => {
    const k = String(row[0] || '').trim();
    if (!k) return;
    let v = row[1];
    if (v instanceof Date) v = v.toISOString();
    out[k] = v;
  });
  return out;
}

function getLatestStvInfo_(ss) {
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName('STV_Update_Log');
  if (!sh) return null;

  const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return null;

  const headers = data[0].map(h => String(h || '').trim());
  const idx = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    idx[String(h || '').trim().toLowerCase()] = i;
  });

  const iRun = idx['stv_run_id'];
  const iSaved = idx['saved_at'];
  const iBy = idx['saved_by'];
  const iVer = idx['stv_version'];

  let best = null;
  for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    const row = data[r];
    const ver = iVer != null ? row[iVer] : '';
    const saved = iSaved != null ? row[iSaved] : '';
    const d = _parseAnyDate_(saved) || _parseAnyDate_(ver);
    if (!d) continue;
    if (!best || d > best.date) {
      best = {
        date: d,
        runId: iRun != null ? String(row[iRun] || '').trim() : '',
        savedAt: iSaved != null ? String(row[iSaved] || '').trim() : '',
        savedBy: iBy != null ? String(row[iBy] || '').trim() : '',
        version: iVer != null ? String(row[iVer] || '').trim() : ''
      };
    }
  }

  if (!best) return null;
  return {
    runId: best.runId,
    savedAt: best.savedAt,
    savedBy: best.savedBy,
    version: best.version || (best.date ? best.date.toISOString() : '')
  };
}



// ------------------------ Helpers (no trailing "_" in names) ------------------------

function srvrGetSheet(ss, name) {
  return ss.getSheetByName(name);
}

function srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh) {
  if (!sh) return [];
  const lr = sh.getLastRow();
  const lc = sh.getLastColumn();
  if (lr < 2 || lc < 1) return [];
  return sh.getRange(2, 1, lr - 1, lc).getValues();
}

function srvrGetHeaders(sh) {
  if (!sh) return [];
  const lc = sh.getLastColumn();
  if (lc < 1) return [];
  return sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, lc).getValues()[0].map(h => String(h || "").trim());
}

function srvrNum(v) {
  if (v === "" || v == null) return null;
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null;
}

function srvrLowerMap(headers) {
  const m = {};
  headers.forEach((h, i) => {
    const k = String(h || "").trim().toLowerCase();
    if (k) m[k] = i;
  });
  return m;
}

// ------------------------ Readers matching your frontend renderers ------------------------

function srvrReadCriteria(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "Criteria");
  if (!sh) return [];
  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);
  const rows = srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);

  // Frontend renderCriteria expects objects: {s,p,m,l,rel,u,re,ac}
  return rows
    .filter(r => r.some(x => String(x || "").trim() !== ""))
    .map(r => ({
      s: String(r[col["spectro"]] || ""),
      p: String(r[col["parameter"]] || ""),
      m: String(r[col["metric"]] || ""),
      l: String(r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit ".trim()]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || r[col["l.limit"]] || ""),
      rel: String(r[col["relation"]] || ""),
      u: String(r[col["u.limit"]] || ""),
      re: String(r[col["reason"]] || ""),
      ac: String(r[col["action"]] || "")
    }));
}

function srvrReadSTV(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "STV");
  if (!sh) return [];
  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);
  const rows = srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);

  // Frontend renderSTV expects objects: {p,a,b,df1,df2,df3,df4}
  return rows
    .filter(r => r.some(x => String(x || "").trim() !== ""))
    .map(r => ({
      p: String(r[col["parameter"]] || ""),
      a: srvrNum(r[col["a"]]),
      b: srvrNum(r[col["b"]]),
      df1: srvrNum(r[col["df1"]]),
      df2: srvrNum(r[col["df2"]]),
      df3: srvrNum(r[col["df3"]]),
      df4: srvrNum(r[col["df4"]])
    }));
}

function srvrReadQCCriteria(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "QC_Criteria");
  if (!sh) return [];
  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);
  const rows = srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);

  // Frontend renderQCCriteria expects objects: {batch,p,min,max,mean}
  // We try to map by common header names; if your headers differ, rename sheet headers to:
  // QC_Batch | Parameter | Min | Max | Mean
  return rows
    .filter(r => r.some(x => String(x || "").trim() !== ""))
    .map(r => ({
      batch: String(r[col["qc_batch"]] || r[col["qc batch"]] || r[col["batch"]] || "Default"),
      p: String(r[col["parameter"]] || r[col["param"]] || ""),
      min: srvrNum(r[col["min"]]),
      max: srvrNum(r[col["max"]]),
      mean: srvrNum(r[col["mean"]])
    }));
}

function srvrReadBlanks(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "Blanks");
  if (!sh) return [];
  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);
  const rows = srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);

  // Frontend renderBlanks expects objects: {p,val}
  // Prefer headers: Parameter | Value
  return rows
    .filter(r => r.some(x => String(x || "").trim() !== ""))
    .map(r => ({
      p: String(r[col["parameter"]] || r[col["param"]] || ""),
      val: srvrNum(r[col["value"]] || r[col["val"]])
    }));
}

function srvrReadRegistryRows(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "Device_Registry");
  if (!sh) return [];
  return srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh); // frontend mapRegistryRow(row) expects raw rows
}

function srvrReadServiceConfig(ss) {
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "Service_Config");
  if (!sh) return []; // frontend already has a fallback mock in your rough file
  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);
  const rows = srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);

  // processServiceConfig expects items like:
  // { section, btn, sub, act, subActions }
  return rows
    .filter(r => r.some(x => String(x || "").trim() !== ""))
    .map(r => ({
      section: String(r[col["section"]] || ""),
      btn: String(r[col["btn"]] || r[col["button"]] || ""),
      sub: String(r[col["sub"]] || ""),
      act: String(r[col["act"]] || r[col["action"]] || ""),
      subActions: String(r[col["subactions"]] || r[col["sub_actions"]] || r[col["sub actions"]] || "")
    }));
}

function srvrReadRecipeConfig(ss) {
  // Supports either:
  // 1) Sheet "Recipe_Config" with JSON in A1
  // 2) Tabular rows which frontend can still interpret if you later code it
  const sh = srvrGetSheet(ss, "Recipe_Config");
  if (!sh) return null;

  const a1 = String(sh.getRange(1, 1).getValue() || "").trim();
  if (a1 && (a1[0] === "{" || a1[0] === "[")) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(a1);
    } catch (e) {
      // If JSON is malformed, return null so frontend fallback can apply
      return null;
    }
  }

  // If not JSON, return raw rows (excluding header)
  return srvrGetValuesNoHeader(sh);
}


// =============================
// MODULE 4: OPERATIONAL DATA
// =============================
function getOperationalData() {
  const SAFE = { rgbData: [], qcData: [], __status: 'OK' };

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (!ss) throw new Error('No active spreadsheet bound to script');

    // RGB_Data is expected; QC_Data is optional (depending on your deployment state)
    const rgbSheet = ss.getSheetByName('RGB_Data');
    if (!rgbSheet) throw new Error('Missing required sheet: RGB_Data');

    const qcSheet = ss.getSheetByName('QC_Data'); // optional

    // UNLIMITED DATA LOAD (as you want)
    const rgbData = getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'RGB_Data', false) || [];
    let qcData = [];
    if (qcSheet) {
      qcData = getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'QC_Data', true) || [];
    } else {
      Logger.log('[WARN] QC_Data sheet not found; returning qcData=[]');
    }

    SAFE.rgbData = Array.isArray(rgbData) ? rgbData : [];
    SAFE.qcData = Array.isArray(qcData) ? qcData : [];

    Logger.log(`[getOperationalData] rgb=${SAFE.rgbData.length}, qc=${SAFE.qcData.length}`);
    return SAFE;

  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('[getOperationalData FATAL] ' + e.message);
    return { rgbData: [], qcData: [], __status: 'ERROR', __error: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Return operational data limited to the last N days.
 * Used for fast initial load; full data can be warmed later.
 * IMPORTANT: Includes size monitoring to prevent payload overflow.
 */
function getOperationalDataRecent(days) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const SAFE = { rgbData: [], qcData: [], __status: 'OK', __info: {} };
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (!ss) throw new Error('No active spreadsheet bound to script');

    const nDays = Number(days) || 30;
    const cutoff = new Date();
    cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - nDays);

    const rgbData = getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'RGB_Data', false) || [];
    const qcSheet = ss.getSheetByName('QC_Data');
    const qcData = qcSheet ? (getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'QC_Data', true) || []) : [];

    const isRecent = (row) => {
      if (!row) return false;
      const v = row.Test_date ?? row.time ?? row.Date ?? row.datetime ?? '';
      const d = _parseAnyDate_(v);
      if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;
      return d >= cutoff;
    };

    SAFE.rgbData = rgbData.filter(isRecent);
    SAFE.qcData = qcData.filter(isRecent);
    
    // Add size and performance monitoring
    SAFE.__info = {
      ms: Date.now() - t0,
      days: nDays,
      totalRgb: rgbData.length,
      totalQc: qcData.length,
      filteredRgb: SAFE.rgbData.length,
      filteredQc: SAFE.qcData.length
    };
    
    // Size estimation and warning
    try {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(SAFE);
      const sizeKB = Math.round(serialized.length / 1024);
      SAFE.__info.payloadSizeKB = sizeKB;
      
      if (sizeKB > CONFIG.PAYLOAD_WARNING_KB) { // > 5MB warning
        console.warn('[getOperationalDataRecent] Large payload: ' + sizeKB + 'KB for ' + nDays + ' days');
      }
      
      Logger.log('[getOperationalDataRecent] OK days=' + nDays + 
        ' rgbTotal=' + rgbData.length + ' rgbFiltered=' + SAFE.rgbData.length +
        ' qcTotal=' + qcData.length + ' qcFiltered=' + SAFE.qcData.length +
        ' sizeKB=' + sizeKB + ' ms=' + SAFE.__info.ms);
    } catch (sizeErr) {
      console.error('[getOperationalDataRecent] Size check failed: ' + sizeErr.message);
    }

    return SAFE;
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('[getOperationalDataRecent FATAL] ' + e.message);
    return { rgbData: [], qcData: [], __status: 'ERROR', __error: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Get operational data with pagination support.
 * Returns a page of data with metadata for client-side pagination.
 * @param {Object} options - { page: 1, pageSize: 500, days: 30, sheetType: 'rgb'|'qc'|'both' }
 */
function getOperationalDataPaginated(options) {
  const t0 = Date.now();
  const page = Number(options?.page) || 1;
  const pageSize = Math.min(Number(options?.pageSize) || CONFIG.DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE, CONFIG.MAX_PAGE_SIZE);
  const days = Number(options?.days) || CONFIG.DEFAULT_RECENT_DAYS;
  const sheetType = options?.sheetType || 'both';
  
  // Validate sheetType
  if (!['rgb', 'qc', 'both'].includes(sheetType)) {
    Logger.log('[getOperationalDataPaginated] Invalid sheetType: ' + sheetType);
    return { 
      rgbData: [], 
      qcData: [], 
      __status: 'ERROR', 
      __error: 'Invalid sheetType: must be rgb, qc, or both'
    };
  }
  
  const SAFE = { 
    rgbData: [], 
    qcData: [], 
    __status: 'OK', 
    __pagination: {
      page,
      pageSize,
      totalPages: 0,
      totalRecords: 0,
      totalRgbRecords: 0,
      totalQcRecords: 0,
      hasMore: false
    },
    __info: {}
  };
  
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    if (!ss) throw new Error('No active spreadsheet bound to script');

    const cutoff = new Date();
    cutoff.setDate(cutoff.getDate() - days);

    const isRecent = (row) => {
      if (!row) return false;
      const v = row.Test_date ?? row.time ?? row.Date ?? row.datetime ?? '';
      const d = _parseAnyDate_(v);
      if (!d || isNaN(d.getTime())) return false;
      return d >= cutoff;
    };
    
    // Helper to calculate pagination metadata
    const calcPagination = (totalRecords) => ({
      totalRecords,
      totalPages: Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize),
      hasMore: page < Math.ceil(totalRecords / pageSize)
    });

    // Load RGB data if requested
    if (sheetType === 'rgb' || sheetType === 'both') {
      const rgbData = getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'RGB_Data', false) || [];
      const filtered = rgbData.filter(isRecent);
      
      const startIdx = (page - 1) * pageSize;
      const endIdx = startIdx + pageSize;
      SAFE.rgbData = filtered.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
      
      const paginationInfo = calcPagination(filtered.length);
      SAFE.__pagination.totalRgbRecords = paginationInfo.totalRecords;
      if (sheetType === 'rgb') {
        SAFE.__pagination.totalRecords = paginationInfo.totalRecords;
        SAFE.__pagination.totalPages = paginationInfo.totalPages;
        SAFE.__pagination.hasMore = paginationInfo.hasMore;
      }
    }

    // Load QC data if requested
    if (sheetType === 'qc' || sheetType === 'both') {
      const qcSheet = ss.getSheetByName('QC_Data');
      if (qcSheet) {
        const qcData = getRGBDataFormatted_(ss, 'QC_Data', true) || [];
        const filtered = qcData.filter(isRecent);
        
        const startIdx = (page - 1) * pageSize;
        const endIdx = startIdx + pageSize;
        SAFE.qcData = filtered.slice(startIdx, endIdx);
        
        const paginationInfo = calcPagination(filtered.length);
        SAFE.__pagination.totalQcRecords = paginationInfo.totalRecords;
        if (sheetType === 'qc') {
          SAFE.__pagination.totalRecords = paginationInfo.totalRecords;
          SAFE.__pagination.totalPages = paginationInfo.totalPages;
          SAFE.__pagination.hasMore = paginationInfo.hasMore;
        }
      }
    }
    
    // For 'both' mode, combine totals
    if (sheetType === 'both') {
      const combinedTotal = SAFE.__pagination.totalRgbRecords + SAFE.__pagination.totalQcRecords;
      SAFE.__pagination.totalRecords = combinedTotal;
      SAFE.__pagination.totalPages = Math.ceil(combinedTotal / pageSize);
      SAFE.__pagination.hasMore = page < SAFE.__pagination.totalPages;
    }
    
    SAFE.__info = {
      ms: Date.now() - t0,
      days,
      page,
      pageSize
    };
    
    // Size check
    try {
      const serialized = JSON.stringify(SAFE);
      const sizeKB = Math.round(serialized.length / 1024);
      SAFE.__info.payloadSizeKB = sizeKB;
      
      Logger.log('[getOperationalDataPaginated] OK page=' + page + '/' + SAFE.__pagination.totalPages +
        ' records=' + SAFE.__pagination.totalRecords + ' sizeKB=' + sizeKB + ' ms=' + SAFE.__info.ms);
    } catch (sizeErr) {
      console.error('[getOperationalDataPaginated] Size check failed: ' + sizeErr.message);
    }

    return SAFE;
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('[getOperationalDataPaginated FATAL] ' + e.message);
    return { 
      rgbData: [], 
      qcData: [], 
      __status: 'ERROR', 
      __error: e.message,
      __pagination: SAFE.__pagination
    };
  }
}

function syncMoveDataToQC(reqRows) {
  requireRole_('operator');

  const parsed = safeJsonParse_(reqRows, []);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { ok: false, error: parsed.error };
  const items = Array.isArray(parsed.data) ? parsed.data : [];
  if (items.length === 0) return { ok: true, moved: 0, updated: 0, removed: 0, skipped: 0 };
  if (items.length > CONFIG.MAX_BATCH_SIZE) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Batch too large (' + items.length + '). Max is ' + CONFIG.MAX_BATCH_SIZE + ' rows per call.' };
  }

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(CONFIG.LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Server busy — another sync is in progress. Please retry in a moment.' };
  }

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const shSrc = ss.getSheetByName("RGB_Data");
    if (!shSrc) return { ok: false, error: "RGB_Data sheet not found." };

    let shDst = ss.getSheetByName("QC_Data");
    if (!shDst) shDst = ss.insertSheet("QC_Data");

    const srcVals = shSrc.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (srcVals.length < 2) return { ok: false, message: "RGB_Data has no rows." };

    const srcHdr = srcVals[0].map(h => String(h ?? ""));
    const srcIdx = _buildHdrIndex_(srcHdr);

    const sDid = srcIdx["device_id"];
    const sTid = srcIdx["test_id"];
    const sDat = srcIdx["test_date"] ?? srcIdx["date"] ?? srcIdx["datetime"];
    const sBat = srcIdx["qcbatch_id"] ?? srcIdx["qc_batch"];

    if (sDid == null || sTid == null || sDat == null) {
      return { ok: false, message: "RGB_Data must contain Device_ID, Test_ID, Test_date." };
    }

    // --- Ensure QC_Data schema == RGB_Data schema (upgrade if needed) ---
    const wantCols = srcHdr.length;

    const dstLastRow = shDst.getLastRow();
    const dstLastCol = shDst.getLastColumn();

    if (dstLastRow < 1 || dstLastCol < 1) {
      shDst.clear();
      shDst.getRange(1, 1, 1, wantCols).setValues([srcHdr]);
      shDst.setFrozenRows(1);
    } else {
      const dstHdrExisting = shDst
        .getRange(1, 1, 1, Math.max(dstLastCol, wantCols))
        .getValues()[0]
        .map(h => String(h ?? ""));

      const aligned = (dstHdrExisting.length >= wantCols) &&
        (dstHdrExisting.slice(0, wantCols).join("|") === srcHdr.join("|"));

      // Write canonical header
      shDst.getRange(1, 1, 1, wantCols).setValues([srcHdr]);
      shDst.setFrozenRows(1);

      // If sheet previously had fewer cols, pad existing rows
      if (!aligned && dstLastRow >= 2) {
        const oldData = shDst.getRange(2, 1, dstLastRow - 1, dstLastCol).getValues();
        const padded = oldData.map(r => {
          const out = r.slice(0, wantCols);
          while (out.length < wantCols) out.push("");
          return out;
        });
        shDst.getRange(2, 1, padded.length, wantCols).setValues(padded);
      }
    }

    const dstVals = shDst.getDataRange().getValues();
    const dstHdr = dstVals[0].map(h => String(h ?? ""));
    const dstIdx = _buildHdrIndex_(dstHdr);

    const dDid = dstIdx["device_id"];
    const dTid = dstIdx["test_id"];
    const dDat = dstIdx["test_date"] ?? dstIdx["date"] ?? dstIdx["datetime"];
    const dBat = dstIdx["qcbatch_id"] ?? dstIdx["qc_batch"];

    if (dDid == null || dTid == null || dDat == null) {
      return { ok: false, message: "QC_Data must contain Device_ID, Test_ID, Test_date after schema sync." };
    }
    if (dBat == null && sBat == null) return { ok: false, message: "Missing QCBatch_ID column in schema." };

    // ---------- Canonical date key (stable across Date objects and display strings) ----------
    const _canonDateKey = (v) => {
      // Use your existing helper if present
      if (typeof _dateKey_ === "function") {
        const k = _dateKey_(v);
        if (k) return k; // "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm"
      }
      // Fallback: try parse
      if (typeof _parseAnyDate_ === "function") {
        const d = _parseAnyDate_(v);
        if (d && !isNaN(d.getTime())) {
          return Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
        }
      }
      // Last resort: normalize pipe to space and trim
      const s0 = String(v ?? "").trim();
      return s0.includes("|") ? s0.replace(/\s*\|\s*/g, " ").trim() : s0;
    };

    const key3 = (did, tid, dt) =>
      `${String(did ?? "").trim()}|${String(tid ?? "").trim()}|${_canonDateKey(dt)}`;

// Build src map by Device+Test+Date + fallback Device+Test (for date-format mismatches)
    const srcMap = new Map();
    const srcByDidTid = new Map();
    for (let r = 1; r < srcVals.length; r++) {
      const row = srcVals[r];
      const did = String(row[sDid] ?? "").trim();
      const tid = String(row[sTid] ?? "").trim();
      const k = key3(did, tid, row[sDat]);
      if (k && !srcMap.has(k)) srcMap.set(k, { rowNum: r + 1, row });
      const k2 = `${did}|${tid}`;
      if (!srcByDidTid.has(k2)) srcByDidTid.set(k2, []);
      srcByDidTid.get(k2).push({ rowNum: r + 1, row });
    }

    // Build dst map (existing rowNum)
    const dstMap = new Map();
    for (let r = 1; r < dstVals.length; r++) {
      const row = dstVals[r];
      const k = key3(row[dDid], row[dTid], row[dDat]);
      if (k && !dstMap.has(k)) dstMap.set(k, r + 1);
    }

    const appends = [];
    const updates = [];
    const deleteRows = [];
    let moved = 0, updated = 0, skipped = 0;

    items.forEach(req => {
      const did = req?.Device_ID ?? req?.did ?? req?.device_id ?? "";
      const tid = req?.Test_ID ?? req?.tid ?? req?.test_id ?? "";
      const dt = req?.Test_date ?? req?.test_date ?? req?.time ?? req?.date ?? req?.datetime ?? "";

      const batch = String(
        req?.QCBatch_ID ?? req?.qcBatchId ?? req?.QC_Batch ?? req?.batch ?? "Default"
      ).trim() || "Default";

      const k = key3(did, tid, dt);
      let hit = srcMap.get(k);
      if (!hit) {
        const k2 = `${String(did ?? "").trim()}|${String(tid ?? "").trim()}`;
        const candidates = srcByDidTid.get(k2) || [];
        if (candidates.length === 1) hit = candidates[0];
      }
      if (!hit) { skipped++; return; }

      const out = hit.row.slice(0, wantCols);
      while (out.length < wantCols) out.push("");

      // Force batch
      const batCol = (dBat != null) ? dBat : sBat;
      if (batCol != null) out[batCol] = batch;

      const resolvedKey = key3(hit.row[sDid], hit.row[sTid], hit.row[sDat]);
      const existingRowNum = dstMap.get(resolvedKey);
      if (existingRowNum) {
        updates.push({ rowNum: existingRowNum, row: out });
        updated++;
      } else {
        appends.push(out);
        moved++;
      }

      deleteRows.push(hit.rowNum);
    });

    // --- Batch write: apply updates in-memory then flush in one setValues call ---
    if (updates.length || appends.length) {
      // Re-read dstVals after schema sync to get current state
      const currentDstVals = shDst.getDataRange().getValues();
      updates.forEach(u => {
        // rowNum is 1-based; index 0 is header row
        if (u.rowNum - 1 < currentDstVals.length) {
          currentDstVals[u.rowNum - 1] = u.row;
        }
      });
      appends.forEach(row => currentDstVals.push(row));
      // Single write for all rows (excluding header already at index 0)
      if (currentDstVals.length > 1) {
        shDst.getRange(2, 1, currentDstVals.length - 1, wantCols).setValues(currentDstVals.slice(1));
      }
    }

    // Format Test_date display
    shDst.getRange(2, dDat + 1, Math.max(0, shDst.getLastRow() - 1), 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy-mm-dd | HH:mm");

    // CUT from RGB_Data (reverse-order delete is correct GAS pattern for non-contiguous rows)
    deleteRows.sort((a, b) => b - a).forEach(rn => shSrc.deleteRow(rn));

    return { ok: true, moved, updated, removed: deleteRows.length, skipped };

  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, message: String(e?.message || e) };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}



function syncMoveDataToRGB(reqRows) {
  requireRole_('operator');

  const parsed = safeJsonParse_(reqRows, []);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { ok: false, error: parsed.error };
  const items = Array.isArray(parsed.data) ? parsed.data : [];
  if (items.length === 0) return { ok: true, moved: 0, updated: 0, removed: 0, skipped: 0 };
  if (items.length > CONFIG.MAX_BATCH_SIZE) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Batch too large (' + items.length + '). Max is ' + CONFIG.MAX_BATCH_SIZE + ' rows per call.' };
  }

  const lock = LockService.getScriptLock();
  if (!lock.tryLock(CONFIG.LOCK_TIMEOUT_MS)) {
    return { ok: false, error: 'Server busy — another sync is in progress. Please retry in a moment.' };
  }

  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const shSrc = ss.getSheetByName("QC_Data");
    const shDst = ss.getSheetByName("RGB_Data");
    if (!shSrc || !shDst) return { ok: false, error: "Missing QC_Data or RGB_Data sheet." };

    const dstVals = shDst.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (dstVals.length < 1) return { ok: false, message: "RGB_Data has no header row." };

    const dstHdr = dstVals[0].map(h => String(h ?? ""));
    const dIdx = _buildHdrIndex_(dstHdr);

    const dDid = dIdx["device_id"];
    const dTid = dIdx["test_id"];
    const dDat = dIdx["test_date"] ?? dIdx["date"] ?? dIdx["datetime"];

    if (dDid == null || dTid == null || dDat == null) {
      return { ok: false, message: "RGB_Data must contain Device_ID, Test_ID, Test_date." };
    }

    const srcVals = shSrc.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (srcVals.length < 2) return { ok: true, moved: 0, updated: 0, removed: 0, skipped: items.length };

    const srcHdr = srcVals[0].map(h => String(h ?? ""));
    const sIdx = _buildHdrIndex_(srcHdr);

    const sDid = sIdx["device_id"];
    const sTid = sIdx["test_id"];
    const sDat = sIdx["test_date"] ?? sIdx["date"] ?? sIdx["datetime"];

    if (sDid == null || sTid == null || sDat == null) {
      return { ok: false, message: "QC_Data must contain Device_ID, Test_ID, Test_date." };
    }

    // ---------- Canonical date key ----------
    const _canonDateKey = (v) => {
      if (typeof _dateKey_ === "function") {
        const k = _dateKey_(v);
        if (k) return k;
      }
      if (typeof _parseAnyDate_ === "function") {
        const d = _parseAnyDate_(v);
        if (d && !isNaN(d.getTime())) {
          return Utilities.formatDate(d, Session.getScriptTimeZone(), "yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm");
        }
      }
      const s0 = String(v ?? "").trim();
      return s0.includes("|") ? s0.replace(/\s*\|\s*/g, " ").trim() : s0;
    };

    const key3 = (did, tid, dt) =>
      `${String(did ?? "").trim()}|${String(tid ?? "").trim()}|${_canonDateKey(dt)}`;

    // Build QC map + fallback Device+Test map (for date-format mismatches)
    const srcMap = new Map();
    const srcByDidTid = new Map();
    for (let r = 1; r < srcVals.length; r++) {
      const row = srcVals[r];
      const did = String(row[sDid] ?? "").trim();
      const tid = String(row[sTid] ?? "").trim();
      const k = key3(did, tid, row[sDat]);
      if (k && !srcMap.has(k)) srcMap.set(k, { rowNum: r + 1, row });
      const k2 = `${did}|${tid}`;
      if (!srcByDidTid.has(k2)) srcByDidTid.set(k2, []);
      srcByDidTid.get(k2).push({ rowNum: r + 1, row });
    }

    // Build RGB existing key->rowNum
    const dstKeyToRowNum = new Map();
    for (let r = 1; r < dstVals.length; r++) {
      const row = dstVals[r];
      const k = key3(row[dDid], row[dTid], row[dDat]);
      if (k && !dstKeyToRowNum.has(k)) dstKeyToRowNum.set(k, r + 1);
    }

    // Map headers by normalized key
    const srcByNorm = {};
    srcHdr.forEach((h, i) => { srcByNorm[_normHdrKey_(h)] = i; });
    const dstByNorm = {};
    dstHdr.forEach((h, i) => { dstByNorm[_normHdrKey_(h)] = i; });

    const wantCols = dstHdr.length;
    const appends = [];
    const updates = [];
    const deleteRows = [];
    let skipped = 0, moved = 0, updated = 0;

    const setIfBlank = (out, normKey, val) => {
      const j = dstByNorm[normKey];
      if (j == null) return;
      if (out[j] === "" || out[j] == null) out[j] = val;
    };

    items.forEach(it => {
      const did = it?.Device_ID ?? it?.did ?? it?.device_id ?? "";
      const tid = it?.Test_ID ?? it?.tid ?? it?.test_id ?? "";
      const dt = it?.Test_date ?? it?.test_date ?? it?.time ?? it?.date ?? it?.datetime ?? "";

      const k = key3(did, tid, dt);
      let hit = srcMap.get(k);
      if (!hit) {
        const k2 = `${String(did ?? "").trim()}|${String(tid ?? "").trim()}`;
        const candidates = srcByDidTid.get(k2) || [];
        if (candidates.length === 1) hit = candidates[0];
      }
      if (!hit) { skipped++; return; }

      const srcRow = hit.row;
      const out = new Array(wantCols).fill("");

      // Copy matching columns by normalized header key
      Object.keys(dstByNorm).forEach(nk => {
        const dj = dstByNorm[nk];
        const si = srcByNorm[nk];
        if (dj != null && si != null) out[dj] = srcRow[si];
      });

      // Force keys from QC row
      out[dDid] = srcRow[sDid];
      out[dTid] = srcRow[sTid];
      out[dDat] = srcRow[sDat];

      // Ensure defaults only if blank
      setIfBlank(out, "client_id", "Default");
      setIfBlank(out, "farm_id", "Default");
      setIfBlank(out, "prodbatch_id", "Default");
      setIfBlank(out, "qcbatch_id", "Default");

      const resolvedKey = key3(srcRow[sDid], srcRow[sTid], srcRow[sDat]);
      const existingRowNum = dstKeyToRowNum.get(resolvedKey);
      if (existingRowNum) {
        updates.push({ rowNum: existingRowNum, row: out });
        updated++;
      } else {
        appends.push(out);
        moved++;
      }

      deleteRows.push(hit.rowNum);
    });

    // --- Batch write: apply updates in-memory then flush in one setValues call ---
    if (updates.length || appends.length) {
      const currentDstVals = shDst.getDataRange().getValues();
      updates.forEach(u => {
        if (u.rowNum - 1 < currentDstVals.length) {
          currentDstVals[u.rowNum - 1] = u.row;
        }
      });
      appends.forEach(row => currentDstVals.push(row));
      if (currentDstVals.length > 1) {
        shDst.getRange(2, 1, currentDstVals.length - 1, wantCols).setValues(currentDstVals.slice(1));
      }
    }

    // Format Test_date display
    shDst.getRange(2, dDat + 1, Math.max(0, shDst.getLastRow() - 1), 1).setNumberFormat("yyyy-mm-dd | HH:mm");

    // CUT from QC_Data (reverse-order delete is correct GAS pattern for non-contiguous rows)
    deleteRows.sort((a, b) => b - a).forEach(rn => shSrc.deleteRow(rn));

    appendDataAudit_('sync_to_qc', 'QC_Data', moved + updated, 'moved=' + moved + ' updated=' + updated + ' skipped=' + skipped);
    return { ok: true, moved, updated, removed: deleteRows.length, skipped };

  } catch (e) {
    return { ok: false, error: String(e?.message || e) };
  } finally {
    lock.releaseLock();
  }
}








function deleteFromQCData(payload) {
  requireRole_('operator');

  const parsed = safeJsonParse_(payload, []);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { ok: false, error: parsed.error };
  const rowsIn = Array.isArray(parsed.data) ? parsed.data : [];
  if (rowsIn.length === 0) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName("QC_Data");
  if (!sh) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);

  const didIdx = col["device_id"];
  const tidIdx = col["test_id"];
  if (didIdx == null || tidIdx == null) throw new Error("QC_Data must contain Device_ID and Test_ID");

  const kill = new Set(
    rowsIn.map(r => {
      const did = String(r.did || r.Device_ID || r.device_id || "").trim();
      const tid = String(r.tid || r.Test_ID || r.test_id || "").trim();
      return (did && tid) ? (did + "|" + tid) : null;
    }).filter(Boolean)
  );

  if (!kill.size) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const data = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues();
  const toDelete = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    const did = String(data[i][didIdx] || "").trim();
    const tid = String(data[i][tidIdx] || "").trim();
    if (kill.has(did + "|" + tid)) toDelete.push(i + 2);
  }

  toDelete.sort((a, b) => b - a).forEach(rn => sh.deleteRow(rn));
  appendDataAudit_('delete_qc', 'QC_Data', toDelete.length, '');
  return { ok: true, deleted: toDelete.length };
}
