function deleteFromRGBData(payload) {
  requireRole_('operator');

  const parsed = safeJsonParse_(payload, []);
  if (!parsed.ok) return { ok: false, error: parsed.error };
  const rowsIn = Array.isArray(parsed.data) ? parsed.data : [];
  if (rowsIn.length === 0) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName("RGB_Data");
  if (!sh) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const headers = srvrGetHeaders(sh);
  const col = srvrLowerMap(headers);

  const didIdx = col["device_id"];
  const tidIdx = col["test_id"];
  if (didIdx == null || tidIdx == null) throw new Error("RGB_Data must contain Device_ID and Test_ID");

  const kill = new Set(
    rowsIn.map(r => {
      const did = String(r.did || r.Device_ID || r.device_id || "").trim();
      const tid = String(r.tid || r.Test_ID || r.test_id || "").trim();
      return (did && tid) ? (did + "|" + tid) : null;
    }).filter(Boolean)
  );

  if (!kill.size) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const lastRow = sh.getLastRow();
  if (lastRow < 2) return { ok: true, deleted: 0 };

  const data = sh.getRange(2, 1, lastRow - 1, sh.getLastColumn()).getValues();
  const toDelete = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < data.length; i++) {
    const did = String(data[i][didIdx] || "").trim();
    const tid = String(data[i][tidIdx] || "").trim();
    if (kill.has(did + "|" + tid)) toDelete.push(i + 2);
  }

  toDelete.sort((a, b) => b - a).forEach(rn => sh.deleteRow(rn));
  appendDataAudit_('delete_rgb', 'RGB_Data', toDelete.length, '');
  return { ok: true, deleted: toDelete.length };
}




function getNutrientManagerConfig(clientId) {
  const cid = clientId || 'Default';
  
  // Try cache first (5 minute TTL)
  const cache = CacheService.getScriptCache();
  const cacheKey = 'nmConfig_' + cid;
  const cached = cache.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) {
    try {
      return JSON.parse(cached);
    } catch (err) {
      Logger.log('getNutrientManagerConfig cache parse error: ' + err.message);
    }
  }
  
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  // Helper: getDataByHeaders returns an array of row arrays; we need indices.
  function loadSheetWithMap(sheetName, requestedHeaders) {
    const rows = getDataByHeaders(ss, sheetName, requestedHeaders) || [];
    // getDataByHeaders already reorders columns to match requestedHeaders,
    // so row[0] is requestedHeaders[0], row[1] is requestedHeaders[1], etc.
    return { headers: requestedHeaders, rows: rows };
  }

  // 1) Rating schemes from SoilFertilityRatings
  const fert = loadSheetWithMap('SoilFertilityRatings', [
    'ClientID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'Parameter', 'ClassName', 'ClassOrder',
    'MinValue', 'MaxValue', 'Unit', 'Notes'
  ]);

  const idxClientF = fert.headers.indexOf('ClientID');
  const idxSchemeF = fert.headers.indexOf('RatingSchemeID');

  const ratingSchemesMap = {};
  fert.rows.forEach(function (row) {
    const rowClient = row[idxClientF] ? String(row[idxClientF]).trim() : '';
    if (rowClient !== cid) return;
    const rs = row[idxSchemeF] ? String(row[idxSchemeF]).trim() : '';
    if (!rs) return;
    ratingSchemesMap[rs] = true;
  });

  const ratingSchemes = Object.keys(ratingSchemesMap).sort().map(function (id) {
    return { id: id, label: id };
  });

  // 2) Crops from NutrientCrops (no idxDefY)
  const cropsSheet = loadSheetWithMap('NutrientCrops', [
    'ClientID', 'CropID', 'CropName', 'Category', 'SeasonType',
    'YieldUnit', 'RootDepthClass', 'ActiveFlag', 'Notes'
  ]);

  const idxClientC = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('ClientID');
  const idxCropId = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('CropID');
  const idxCropName = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('CropName');
  const idxCat = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('Category');
  const idxSeason = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('SeasonType');
  const idxYUnit = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('YieldUnit');
  const idxActive = cropsSheet.headers.indexOf('ActiveFlag');

  const crops = cropsSheet.rows
    .filter(function (row) {
      const rowClient = row[idxClientC] ? String(row[idxClientC]).trim() : '';
      if (rowClient !== cid) return false;
      const active = row[idxActive] ? String(row[idxActive]).toLowerCase().trim() : 'yes';
      return active !== 'no';
    })
    .map(function (row) {
      return {
        cropId: row[idxCropId] ? String(row[idxCropId]).trim() : '',
        name: row[idxCropName] ? String(row[idxCropName]).trim() : '',
        category: row[idxCat] || '',
        seasonType: row[idxSeason] || '',
        // defaultTargetYield is now driven by STCR sheet, so keep blank or drop this field
        defaultTargetYield: '',
        yieldUnit: row[idxYUnit] || 't/ha'
      };
    })
    .filter(function (c) { return c.cropId && c.name; });

  // 3) Methods from NutrientMethods
  const methodsSheet = loadSheetWithMap('NutrientMethods', [
    'ClientID', 'MethodID', 'MethodName', 'Description', 'ActiveFlag'
  ]);

  const idxClientM = methodsSheet.headers.indexOf('ClientID');
  const idxMid = methodsSheet.headers.indexOf('MethodID');
  const idxMName = methodsSheet.headers.indexOf('MethodName');
  const idxMDesc = methodsSheet.headers.indexOf('Description');
  const idxMActive = methodsSheet.headers.indexOf('ActiveFlag');

  const methods = methodsSheet.rows
    .filter(function (row) {
      const rowClient = row[idxClientM] ? String(row[idxClientM]).trim() : '';
      if (rowClient !== cid) return false;
      const active = row[idxMActive] ? String(row[idxMActive]).toLowerCase().trim() : 'yes';
      return active !== 'no';
    })
    .map(function (row) {
      return {
        methodId: row[idxMid] ? String(row[idxMid]).trim() : '',
        name: row[idxMName] ? String(row[idxMName]).trim() : '',
        description: row[idxMDesc] || ''
      };
    })
    .filter(function (m) { return m.methodId && m.name; });

  const result = {
    clientId: cid,
    ratingSchemes: ratingSchemes,
    crops: crops,
    methods: methods
  };
  
  // Cache for 5 minutes (300 seconds)
  try {
    cache.put(cacheKey, JSON.stringify(result), 300);
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('getNutrientManagerConfig cache write error: ' + err.message);
  }
  
  return result;
}


/**
 * Get base dose from NutrientTargets sheet
 * Returns dose information for a specific nutrient based on crop, method, soil class, etc.
 */
function getBaseDoseFromTargets_(clientId, cropId, stageId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, nutrient, soilClass) {
  try {
    const targets = getSheetDataByHeader_('NutrientTargets');
    
    const cClient = targets.getCol('ClientID');
    const cCrop = targets.getCol('CropID');
    const cStage = targets.getCol('StageID');
    const cMethod = targets.getCol('MethodID');
    const cScheme = targets.getCol('RatingSchemeID');
    const cParam = targets.getCol('Parameter');
    const cSoilClass = targets.getCol('SoilClass');
    const cBaseDose = targets.getCol('BaseDosePerHa');
    const cDosePerT = targets.getCol('DosePerTonneYield');
    const cMaxDose = targets.getCol('MaxDosePerHa');
    const cMinDose = targets.getCol('MinDosePerHa');

    // Try to find exact match first
    let row = targets.rows.find(r =>
      String(r[cClient]) === String(clientId) &&
      String(r[cCrop]) === String(cropId) &&
      String(r[cStage]) === String(stageId) &&
      String(r[cMethod]) === String(methodId) &&
      String(r[cScheme]) === String(ratingSchemeId) &&
      String(r[cParam]) === String(nutrient) &&
      String(r[cSoilClass]) === String(soilClass)
    );

    // Fallback: try without stage (aggregate dose)
    if (!row) {
      row = targets.rows.find(r =>
        String(r[cClient]) === String(clientId) &&
        String(r[cCrop]) === String(cropId) &&
        String(r[cMethod]) === String(methodId) &&
        String(r[cScheme]) === String(ratingSchemeId) &&
        String(r[cParam]) === String(nutrient) &&
        (String(r[cStage]) === '' || String(r[cStage]) === 'ALL')
      );
    }

    // Fallback: try Default client
    if (!row) {
      row = targets.rows.find(r =>
        String(r[cClient]) === 'Default' &&
        String(r[cCrop]) === String(cropId) &&
        String(r[cMethod]) === String(methodId) &&
        String(r[cScheme]) === String(ratingSchemeId) &&
        String(r[cParam]) === String(nutrient)
      );
    }

    if (!row) return null;

    return {
      baseDose: Number(row[cBaseDose]) || 0,
      dosePerTonneYield: Number(row[cDosePerT]) || 0,
      maxDose: Number(row[cMaxDose]) || 0,
      minDose: Number(row[cMinDose]) || 0
    };
  } catch (err) {
    Logger.log('getBaseDoseFromTargets_ error: ' + err.message);
    return null;
  }
}


/**
 * Calculate organic nutrient contribution from soil organic carbon (OC%)
 * Returns estimated nutrient contribution in kg/ha from organic matter
 * 
 * Standard assumptions:
 * - 1% OC = 17,241 kg organic matter/ha (top 15cm, bulk density 1.5)
 * - OM contains ~5% N, 0.5% P2O5, 0.5% K2O
 * - Mineralization rates: N=2%, P2O5=1%, K2O=1% per season
 */
function calculateOrganicNutrientContribution_(ocPercent) {
  const oc = Number(ocPercent) || 0;
  if (oc <= 0) return { N: 0, P2O5: 0, K2O: 0 };
  
  // Organic matter (kg/ha) = OC% × 17,241
  // Calculation basis: 15cm soil depth, bulk density 1.5 g/cm³
  // Volume: 10,000 m² × 0.15 m = 1,500 m³ = 1,500,000 L
  // Mass: 1,500,000 L × 1.5 kg/L = 2,250,000 kg soil
  // OM per 1% OC: 2,250,000 kg × 1.72 (van Bemmelen factor) × 0.01 = 17,241 kg/ha
  const organicMatter_kg_ha = oc * 17241;
  
  // Total nutrient content in organic matter
  const totalN = organicMatter_kg_ha * 0.05;      // 5% N in OM
  const totalP = organicMatter_kg_ha * 0.005;     // 0.5% P2O5 in OM
  const totalK = organicMatter_kg_ha * 0.005;     // 0.5% K2O in OM
  
  // Mineralization rate per season (conservative estimates)
  const mineralizationN = 0.02;   // 2% N mineralizes per season
  const mineralizationP = 0.01;   // 1% P mineralizes per season
  const mineralizationK = 0.01;   // 1% K mineralizes per season
  
  return {
    N: Math.round(totalN * mineralizationN * 10) / 10,
    P2O5: Math.round(totalP * mineralizationP * 10) / 10,
    K2O: Math.round(totalK * mineralizationK * 10) / 10
  };
}


/**
 * Standardize nutrient parameter names across the system
 * Maps various parameter names to canonical form (N, P2O5, K2O, S, etc.)
 */
function normalizeNutrientParameter_(param) {
  const p = String(param).trim().toUpperCase();
  
  // Handle common variations
  const mappings = {
    'AVAIL_N': 'N',
    'AVAILABLE_N': 'N',
    'NITROGEN': 'N',
    
    'AVAIL_P': 'P2O5',
    'AVAILABLE_P': 'P2O5',
    'P': 'P2O5',
    'PHOSPHORUS': 'P2O5',
    'P205': 'P2O5',
    
    'AVAIL_K': 'K2O',
    'AVAILABLE_K': 'K2O',
    'K': 'K2O',
    'POTASSIUM': 'K2O',
    'K20': 'K2O',
    
    'AVAIL_S': 'S',
    'AVAILABLE_S': 'S',
    'SULPHUR': 'S',
    'SULFUR': 'S',
    
    'ZN': 'Zn',
    'ZINC': 'Zn',
    
    'FE': 'Fe',
    'IRON': 'Fe',
    
    'MN': 'Mn',
    'MANGANESE': 'Mn',
    
    'CU': 'Cu',
    'COPPER': 'Cu',
    
    'B': 'B',
    'BORON': 'B'
  };
  
  return mappings[p] || param;
}


/**
 * Write calculation details to NutrientDebug sheet for traceability
 */
function writeToNutrientDebug_(testId, calculationDetails) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    let debugSheet = ss.getSheetByName('NutrientDebug');
    
    if (!debugSheet) {
      // Sheet should exist from ensureNutrientConfigSheets, but create if missing
      debugSheet = ss.insertSheet('NutrientDebug');
      debugSheet.getRange(1, 1, 1, 10).setValues([[
        'Timestamp', 'TestID', 'ClientID', 'CropID', 'MethodID', 
        'RatingSchemeID', 'TargetYield', 'CalculationType', 'Details', 'Result'
      ]]);
    }
    
    const timestamp = new Date().toISOString();
    const row = [
      timestamp,
      calculationDetails.testId || testId,
      calculationDetails.clientId || '',
      calculationDetails.cropId || '',
      calculationDetails.methodId || '',
      calculationDetails.ratingSchemeId || '',
      calculationDetails.targetYield || '',
      calculationDetails.calcType || '',
      JSON.stringify(calculationDetails.details || {}),
      JSON.stringify(calculationDetails.result || {})
    ];
    
    debugSheet.appendRow(row);
    
    // Keep only last 1000 rows (to prevent sheet from growing indefinitely)
    // getLastRow() includes header, so if > 1001, delete oldest data rows
    if (debugSheet.getLastRow() > 1001) {
      const rowsToDelete = debugSheet.getLastRow() - 1001;
      debugSheet.deleteRows(2, rowsToDelete);  // Start at row 2 (after header)
    }
  } catch (err) {
    // Don't fail calculation if debug logging fails
    Logger.log('writeToNutrientDebug_ error: ' + err.message);
  }
}


/**
 * Select optimal fertilizer source for a given nutrient requirement
 * Returns best source based on cost, availability, and suitability
 * 
 * @param {Array} availableSources - List of fertilizer sources
 * @param {string} nutrient - Target nutrient (N, P2O5, K2O, S, etc.)
 * @param {number} requiredDose - Required nutrient dose in kg/ha
 * @param {string} stage - Application stage (BASAL, VEG, etc.)
 * @param {string} method - Application method (SOIL, FERTIGATION, FOLIAR)
 * @returns {Object} - {source, quantity_kg_ha, cost_per_ha, secondary_nutrients}
 */
function selectOptimalFertilizerSource_(availableSources, nutrient, requiredDose, stage, method) {
  if (!availableSources || availableSources.length === 0 || requiredDose <= 0) {
    return null;
  }
  
  // Filter sources suitable for this stage and method
  const suitableSources = availableSources.filter(function(src) {
    // Check if source is suitable for the method
    const methods = String(src.suitableMethods || '').toUpperCase();
    if (methods && !methods.includes(method.toUpperCase())) {
      return false;
    }
    
    // Check if source has non-zero grade for the nutrient
    const grade = Number(src[nutrient]) || 0;
    return grade > 0;
  });
  
  if (suitableSources.length === 0) {
    return null;
  }
  
  // Score each source and select the best
  let bestSource = null;
  let bestScore = -Infinity;
  
  suitableSources.forEach(function(src) {
    // Nutrient grade is stored as percentage (e.g., 46 for 46% N in Urea)
    // Convert to decimal for quantity calculation
    const grade = Number(src[nutrient]) / 100;  // e.g., 46% → 0.46
    const quantity = requiredDose / grade;  // kg product per ha
    const pricePerUnit = Number(src.pricePerUnit) || 0;
    const unitSize = Number(src.unitSize) || 1;
    const costPerHa = pricePerUnit * (quantity / unitSize);
    
    // Calculate score (higher is better)
    // Factors: lower cost (60%), higher grade (30%), stage preference (10%)
    let score = 0;
    
    // Cost factor (lower cost = higher score)
    if (pricePerUnit > 0) {
      score -= costPerHa * 0.6;  // Negative because lower cost is better
    }
    
    // Grade factor (higher grade = less product to handle)
    score += (Number(src[nutrient]) || 0) * 0.3;
    
    // Stage preference (exact match gets bonus)
    const srcStage = String(src.defaultStage || '').toUpperCase();
    if (srcStage === stage.toUpperCase()) {
      score += 10;
    }
    
    // Prefer straight sources for single nutrients
    if (src.type === 'Straight') {
      score += 5;
    }
    
    if (score > bestScore) {
      bestScore = score;
      bestSource = {
        source: src,
        quantity_kg_ha: Math.round(quantity * 10) / 10,
        cost_per_ha: Math.round(costPerHa * 100) / 100,
        nutrient_grade: Number(src[nutrient]) || 0,
        secondary_nutrients: (function() {
          // Calculate secondary nutrients from complex fertilizers
          // All nutrient grades are stored as percentages and converted to kg/ha
          const nutrients = {};
          ['N', 'P2O5', 'K2O', 'S', 'Zn', 'Fe', 'Cu', 'Mn', 'B'].forEach(function(nut) {
            const grade = Number(src[nut]) || 0;
            nutrients[nut] = grade > 0 ? Math.round(quantity * (grade / 100) * 10) / 10 : 0;
          });
          return nutrients;
        })()
      };
    }
  });
  
  return bestSource;
}


/**
 * Generate complete fertilizer recommendation with product selection and costing
 * 
 * @param {Object} nutrientPlan - Output from getNutrientPlanForTest
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @returns {Object} - Enhanced plan with fertilizer products and costs
 */
function generateFertilizerRecommendation_(nutrientPlan, clientId) {
  if (!nutrientPlan || !nutrientPlan.stages || nutrientPlan.stages.length === 0) {
    return {
      fertilizerSchedule: [],
      totalCost: 0,
      warnings: ['No nutrient plan available']
    };
  }
  
  const sources = getNutrientSources_(clientId || 'Default');
  const fertilizerSchedule = [];
  let totalCost = 0;
  const warnings = [];
  const secondaryContributions = {};  // Track incidental nutrients from complexes
  
  // Group stages by stage name and nutrient
  const stageMap = {};
  nutrientPlan.stages.forEach(function(stage) {
    const key = stage.stage + '_' + stage.nutrient;
    if (!stageMap[key]) {
      stageMap[key] = stage;
    }
  });
  
  // For each stage-nutrient combination, select fertilizer
  Object.keys(stageMap).forEach(function(key) {
    const stage = stageMap[key];
    const dose = Number(stage.dose) || 0;
    
    if (dose <= 0) return;
    
    // Normalize nutrient key (handle Unicode subscripts in P₂O₅, K₂O)
    // Note: This handles ₂ and ₅ subscripts; if other subscripts appear, extend this mapping
    let nutrientKey = stage.nutrient.replace(/₂/g, '2').replace(/₅/g, '5').replace(/₀/g, '0');
    
    // Select optimal source
    const selection = selectOptimalFertilizerSource_(
      sources,
      nutrientKey,
      dose,
      stage.stage,
      stage.method || 'SOIL'
    );
    
    if (selection) {
      fertilizerSchedule.push({
        stage: stage.stage,
        nutrient: stage.nutrient,
        requiredDose_kg_ha: dose,
        fertilizer: selection.source.name,
        fertilizerType: selection.source.type,
        grade: selection.nutrient_grade + '%',
        quantity_kg_ha: selection.quantity_kg_ha,
        units: Math.ceil(selection.quantity_kg_ha / (Number(selection.source.unitSize) || 1)),
        unitSize: selection.source.unitSize,
        unitBasis: selection.source.unitBasis,
        pricePerUnit: selection.source.pricePerUnit,
        cost_per_ha: selection.cost_per_ha,
        currency: selection.source.currency || 'INR',
        method: stage.method || 'SOIL',
        secondaryNutrients: selection.secondary_nutrients
      });
      
      totalCost += selection.cost_per_ha;
      
      // Track secondary nutrients for accounting
      Object.keys(selection.secondary_nutrients).forEach(function(nut) {
        if (nut !== nutrientKey && selection.secondary_nutrients[nut] > 0) {
          if (!secondaryContributions[stage.stage]) {
            secondaryContributions[stage.stage] = {};
          }
          if (!secondaryContributions[stage.stage][nut]) {
            secondaryContributions[stage.stage][nut] = 0;
          }
          secondaryContributions[stage.stage][nut] += selection.secondary_nutrients[nut];
        }
      });
    } else {
      warnings.push('No suitable fertilizer found for ' + stage.nutrient + ' at ' + stage.stage + ' stage');
    }
  });
  
  return {
    fertilizerSchedule: fertilizerSchedule,
    totalCost: Math.round(totalCost * 100) / 100,
    secondaryContributions: secondaryContributions,
    warnings: warnings
  };
}


/**
 * Validate nutrient manager inputs
 * Returns {valid: boolean, errors: string[]}
 */
function validateNutrientManagerInputs_(input) {
  const errors = [];
  
  if (!input) {
    errors.push('Input is null or undefined');
    return { valid: false, errors };
  }
  
  if (!input.testId || String(input.testId).trim() === '') {
    errors.push('Test ID is required');
  }
  
  if (!input.clientId || String(input.clientId).trim() === '') {
    errors.push('Client ID is required');
  }
  
  if (!input.ratingSchemeId || String(input.ratingSchemeId).trim() === '') {
    errors.push('Rating Scheme ID is required');
  }
  
  if (!input.methodId || String(input.methodId).trim() === '') {
    errors.push('Method ID is required');
  }
  
  if (!input.cropId || String(input.cropId).trim() === '') {
    errors.push('Crop ID is required');
  }
  
  // Target yield validation (required for STCR method)
  if (input.methodId && String(input.methodId).toUpperCase().indexOf('STCR') >= 0) {
    const ty = Number(input.targetYield);
    if (isNaN(ty) || ty <= 0) {
      errors.push('Target yield is required and must be > 0 for STCR method');
    }
  }
  
  return {
    valid: errors.length === 0,
    errors
  };
}


/**
 * Generate nutrient fertilization plan for a soil test.
 * 
 * DATA SOURCE: SoilTest_Results sheet is the SOLE source of soil test data.
 * All soil parameters (pH, EC, OC, Avail_N, Avail_P, Avail_K, Avail_S, Zn, Fe, Mn, Cu, B)
 * are retrieved from SoilTest_Results using Test_ID as the primary key.
 * 
 * Other sheets (SoilFertilityRatings, NutrientTargets, NutrientSTCR, etc.) provide
 * calculation parameters and configuration, NOT soil test measurements.
 * 
 * @param {string} testId - Test ID from SoilTest_Results sheet
 * @param {string} deviceId - Device ID (optional, for reference)
 * @param {string} clientId - Client identifier
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme (e.g., 'LMH')
 * @param {string} methodId - Calculation method ('RDF', 'STCR', or application method)
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop name
 * @param {number} targetYield - Target yield in t/ha (required for STCR)
 * @returns {Object} Nutrient plan with summary, stages, and totals
 */
function getNutrientPlanForTest(testId, deviceId, clientId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, cropId, targetYield) {
  // Validate inputs
  const validation = validateNutrientManagerInputs_({
    testId, clientId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, cropId, targetYield
  });
  
  if (!validation.valid) {
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTest validation failed: ' + JSON.stringify(validation.errors));
    return {
      success: false,
      errors: validation.errors,
      summary: [],
      stages: [],
      planTotals: { N: 0, P: 0, K: 0 }
    };
  }

  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  ensureSoilTestResultsSheet_();
  // SOLE DATA SOURCE: Read all soil test parameters from SoilTest_Results sheet
  const sh = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
  ensureColumnWithDefault_(sh, 1, 'Crop', 'Wheat');
  const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return { summary: [], stages: [], planTotals: { N: 0, P: 0, K: 0 } };

  const headers = data[0].map(String);
  const idxTestId = getHeaderIndex_(headers, 'Test_ID');
  const idxClient = headers.indexOf('ClientID');
  const idxCrop = headers.indexOf('Crop');

  let rowObj = null;
  for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
    if (String(data[r][idxTestId]) === String(testId)) {
      rowObj = {};
      headers.forEach((h, i) => rowObj[h] = data[r][i]);
      break;
    }
  }
  if (!rowObj) {
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTest: Test ID not found in SoilTest_Results: ' + testId);
    return {
      success: false,
      errors: ['Test ID ' + testId + ' not found in SoilTest_Results sheet'],
      summary: [],
      stages: [],
      planTotals: { N: 0, P: 0, K: 0 }
    };
  }

  // Fallbacks from sheet if UI didn’t pass them
  const effectiveClientId = clientId || rowObj['ClientID'] || 'Default';
  const effectiveCropId = cropId || rowObj['Crop'];

  // Calculate organic nutrient contribution from OC%
  const ocPercent = Number(rowObj['OC']) || 0;
  const organicContribution = calculateOrganicNutrientContribution_(ocPercent);
  Logger.log('Organic contribution from OC=' + ocPercent + '%: ' + JSON.stringify(organicContribution));

  // Rating schemes table
  const fert = getDataByHeaders(ss, 'SoilFertilityRatings', [
    'ClientID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'Parameter', 'ClassName', 'ClassOrder',
    'MinValue', 'MaxValue', 'Unit', 'Notes', 'DoseMultiplier'
  ]) || [];

  const summary = [];
  ['pH', 'EC', 'OC', 'Avail_N', 'Avail_P', 'Avail_K', 'Avail_S', 'Zn', 'Fe', 'Mn', 'Cu', 'B'].forEach(paramCol => {
    const soilVal = rowObj[paramCol];
    if (soilVal === '' || soilVal === null || soilVal === undefined) return;
    let paramName = paramCol.replace('Avail_', ''); // pH, EC, OC, N, P, K, S, Zn, Fe, Mn, Cu, B

    // Map to actual Parameter names in SoilFertilityRatings using standardized mapping
    const ratingParamKey = normalizeNutrientParameter_(paramName);

    const matches = fert.filter(r =>
      String(r[0]) === String(effectiveClientId) &&
      String(r[1]) === String(ratingSchemeId) &&
      String(r[2]) === String(ratingParamKey)
    );

    let className = '', unit = '', notes = '', doseMultiplier = 1;
    matches.forEach(r => {
      const min = r[5], max = r[6];
      if ((min === '' || soilVal >= min) && (max === '' || soilVal <= max)) {
        className = r[3];
        unit = r[7];
        notes = r[8];
        doseMultiplier = Number(r[9]) || 1;  // Get DoseMultiplier from SoilFertilityRatings
      }
    });

    Logger.log('Nutrient %s: soilVal=%s className=%s doseMultiplier=%s', ratingParamKey, soilVal, className, doseMultiplier);

    // Get baseDose from NutrientTargets sheet (with fallback to hardcoded values)
    let baseDose = 0;
    let dosePerTonne = 0;
    const targetInfo = getBaseDoseFromTargets_(
      effectiveClientId,
      effectiveCropId,
      'ALL',  // For summary, use aggregate dose across all stages
      methodId || 'SOIL',
      ratingSchemeId,
      ratingParamKey,
      className || 'Medium'
    );
    
    if (targetInfo) {
      baseDose = targetInfo.baseDose;
      dosePerTonne = targetInfo.dosePerTonneYield;
    } else {
      // Fallback to default values if NutrientTargets not configured
      if (paramName === 'N') baseDose = 120;
      if (paramName === 'P') baseDose = 60;
      if (paramName === 'K') baseDose = 120;
    }

    summary.push({
      nutrient: paramName,
      soilValue: soilVal,
      unit: unit || '-',
      className: className || '-',
      notes: notes || '',
      baseDose: baseDose,
      doseMultiplier: doseMultiplier  // Use actual multiplier from SoilFertilityRatings
    });
  });

  Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTest summary: ' + JSON.stringify(summary));

  // Determine if we should use STCR or RDF calculation
  // Check both methodId parameter and NutrientDoseEngine sheet configuration
  const useSTCR = methodId && (String(methodId).toUpperCase().indexOf('STCR') >= 0);
  
  Logger.log('Method detection: methodId=%s useSTCR=%s targetYield=%s', methodId, useSTCR, targetYield);
  
  let stages;
  
  if (useSTCR && targetYield && Number(targetYield) > 0) {
    // STCR Mode: Use getFinalNutrientRecommendations for proper STCR calculation
    Logger.log('Using STCR calculation mode with targetYield=' + targetYield);
    
    // Build soilValuesByParam from summary
    const soilValuesByParam = {};
    const organicIndexByParam = {};
    
    summary.forEach(function(s) {
      let key = s.nutrient;
      if (key === 'P') key = 'P2O5';
      if (key === 'K') key = 'K2O';
      
      soilValuesByParam[key] = s.soilValue;
      
      // Use organic contribution for STCR
      if (key === 'N' && organicContribution.N) {
        organicIndexByParam[key] = organicContribution.N;
      } else if (key === 'P2O5' && organicContribution.P2O5) {
        organicIndexByParam[key] = organicContribution.P2O5;
      } else if (key === 'K2O' && organicContribution.K2O) {
        organicIndexByParam[key] = organicContribution.K2O;
      } else {
        organicIndexByParam[key] = 0;
      }
    });
    
    // Get stage-wise doses using STCR
    stages = buildStagesWithSTCR_(
      summary,
      effectiveCropId,
      effectiveClientId,
      methodId,
      ratingSchemeId,
      targetYield,
      soilValuesByParam,
      organicIndexByParam
    );
    
    // Fallback to RDF if STCR returns no results (sheets not configured)
    if (!stages || stages.length === 0) {
      Logger.log('WARNING: STCR returned no results. Falling back to RDF mode. Check NutrientSTCR sheet configuration.');
      stages = buildGeneralStages_(
        summary,
        effectiveCropId,
        effectiveClientId,
        methodId,
        ratingSchemeId,
        targetYield
      );
    }
  } else {
    // RDF Mode: Use traditional buildGeneralStages_
    Logger.log('Using RDF calculation mode');
    stages = buildGeneralStages_(
      summary,
      effectiveCropId,
      effectiveClientId,
      methodId,
      ratingSchemeId,
      targetYield
    );
  }

  Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTest stages.length=' + stages.length);

  // Compute total N, P, K (kg/ha) across all stages
  var totalNkgPerHa = 0;
  var totalPkgPerHa = 0;
  var totalKkgPerHa = 0;

  stages.forEach(function (st) {
    if (st.nRate) totalNkgPerHa += Number(st.nRate) || 0;
    if (st.pRate) totalPkgPerHa += Number(st.pRate) || 0;
    if (st.kRate) totalKkgPerHa += Number(st.kRate) || 0;
  });

  const result = {
    summary: summary,
    stages: stages,
    planTotals: {
      N: totalNkgPerHa,
      P: totalPkgPerHa,
      K: totalKkgPerHa
    }
  };

  // Write calculation details to debug sheet
  writeToNutrientDebug_(testId, {
    testId: testId,
    clientId: effectiveClientId,
    cropId: effectiveCropId,
    methodId: methodId,
    ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
    targetYield: targetYield,
    calcType: 'getNutrientPlanForTest',
    details: {
      organicContribution: organicContribution,
      stageCount: stages.length,
      summaryCount: summary.length
    },
    result: result.planTotals
  });

  return result;
}


/**
 * Build stages using STCR calculation method
 * This function calls getFinalNutrientRecommendations for each stage to get STCR-based doses
 */
function buildStagesWithSTCR_(summary, cropId, clientId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield, soilValuesByParam, organicIndexByParam) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ inputs: clientId=%s cropId=%s methodId=%s ratingSchemeId=%s targetYield=%s',
    clientId, cropId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield);
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ soilValuesByParam: ' + JSON.stringify(soilValuesByParam));
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ organicIndexByParam: ' + JSON.stringify(organicIndexByParam));
  
  // Get stage information
  const stSheet = ss.getSheetByName('NutrientStages');
  const stageRows = [];
  const nFrac = {}, pFrac = {}, kFrac = {}, sFrac = {};
  const orderedStages = [];

  if (stSheet) {
    const stData = stSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (stData.length > 1) {
      const h = stData[0].map(String);
      const idxClient = h.indexOf('ClientID');
      const idxCrop = h.indexOf('CropID');
      const idxStageId = h.indexOf('StageID');
      const idxStageName = h.indexOf('StageName');
      const idxSeq = h.indexOf('SequenceNo');
      const idxNFrac = h.indexOf('N_FractionPct');
      const idxPFrac = h.indexOf('P2O5_FractionPct');
      const idxKFrac = h.indexOf('K2O_FractionPct');
      const idxSFrac = h.indexOf('S_FractionPct');

      for (let r = 1; r < stData.length; r++) {
        const row = stData[r];
        if (String(row[idxClient]) === String(clientId) &&
          String(row[idxCrop]) === String(cropId)) {
          const stageName = String(row[idxStageName] || row[idxStageId] || '').trim();
          const stageId = String(row[idxStageId] || '').trim();
          const seq = Number(row[idxSeq] || 0);
          stageRows.push({
            stageId: stageId,
            stageName: stageName,
            seq: seq,
            nPct: Number(row[idxNFrac] || 0),
            pPct: Number(row[idxPFrac] || 0),
            kPct: Number(row[idxKFrac] || 0),
            sPct: Number(row[idxSFrac] || 0)
          });
        }
      }

      stageRows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.seq - b.seq; });
      stageRows.forEach(function (st) {
        if (!st.stageName) return;
        orderedStages.push(st);
        if (st.nPct) nFrac[st.stageName] = st.nPct / 100;
        if (st.pPct) pFrac[st.stageName] = st.pPct / 100;
        if (st.kPct) kFrac[st.stageName] = st.kPct / 100;
        if (st.sPct) sFrac[st.stageName] = st.sPct / 100;
      });
    }
  }

  if (orderedStages.length === 0) {
    orderedStages.push({ stageId: 'BASAL', stageName: 'Basal', seq: 1 });
    nFrac['Basal'] = 1.0;
    pFrac['Basal'] = 1.0;
    kFrac['Basal'] = 1.0;
    Logger.log('No matching NutrientStages rows; using fallback single stage.');
  }

  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ orderedStages: ' + JSON.stringify(orderedStages));

  // Step 1: Calculate TOTAL dose for each nutrient using STCR (once, not per stage)
  // Use a generic stageID for total calculation (will distribute to stages later)
  const stcrInput = {
    clientID: clientId,
    cropID: cropId,
    stageID: 'ALL',  // Generic stage for total calculation
    methodID: methodId,
    ratingSchemeID: ratingSchemeId,
    targetYield: targetYield,
    soilValuesByParam: soilValuesByParam,
    organicIndexByParam: organicIndexByParam
  };
  
  const totalRecommendations = getFinalNutrientRecommendations(stcrInput);
  
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ total STCR recommendations: ' + JSON.stringify(totalRecommendations));
  
  if (!totalRecommendations || totalRecommendations.length === 0) {
    Logger.log('WARNING: No STCR recommendations returned. Check if NutrientSTCR or NutrientDoseEngine sheets are configured.');
    return [];
  }

  const stages = [];
  
  // Step 2: Distribute total doses across stages using fractions
  totalRecommendations.forEach(function(rec) {
    const nutrient = rec.nutrient;
    const totalDose = rec.dose_kg_ha;
    
    Logger.log('Processing nutrient %s with total dose %s kg/ha', nutrient, totalDose);
    
    // Determine which fraction map to use
    const fracMap = nutrient === 'N' ? nFrac :
                    nutrient === 'P2O5' ? pFrac :
                    nutrient === 'K2O' ? kFrac :
                    nutrient === 'S' ? sFrac : null;
    
    if (!fracMap) {
      Logger.log('No fraction map found for nutrient: ' + nutrient);
      return;
    }
    
    // Create an entry for each stage that has a non-zero fraction
    orderedStages.forEach(function(stageInfo) {
      const stageName = stageInfo.stageName;
      const frac = fracMap[stageName] || 0;
      
      if (frac > 0) {
        const stageDose = Math.round(totalDose * frac * 10) / 10;
        
        Logger.log('Stage %s: %s dose = %s * %s = %s kg/ha', stageName, nutrient, totalDose, frac, stageDose);
        
        let displayLabel = nutrient;
        if (displayLabel === 'P2O5') displayLabel = 'P₂O₅';
        if (displayLabel === 'K2O') displayLabel = 'K₂O';
        
        stages.push({
          stage: stageName,
          nutrient: displayLabel,
          timing: '',
          method: '',
          dose: stageDose,
          unit: 'kg/ha',
          sourceMix: '',
          productQty: '',
          costPerHa: '',
          details: 'STCR: ' + rec.doseEngine + ', Total=' + totalDose + ', Fraction=' + (frac * 100).toFixed(0) + '%',
          flags: ''
        });
      }
    });
  });

  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCR_ returning stages.length=' + stages.length);
  return stages;
}


function buildGeneralStages_(summary, cropId, clientId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();

  Logger.log('buildGeneralStages_ inputs: clientId=%s cropId=%s methodId=%s ratingSchemeId=%s targetYield=%s',
    clientId, cropId, methodId, ratingSchemeId, targetYield);
  Logger.log('buildGeneralStages_ summary: ' + JSON.stringify(summary));

  const stSheet = ss.getSheetByName('NutrientStages');
  const stageRows = [];
  const nFrac = {}, pFrac = {}, kFrac = {}, sFrac = {};
  const orderedStages = [];

  if (stSheet) {
    const stData = stSheet.getDataRange().getValues();
    Logger.log('NutrientStages header row: ' + JSON.stringify(stData[0]));
    if (stData.length > 1) {
      const h = stData[0].map(String);
      const idxClient = h.indexOf('ClientID');
      const idxCrop = h.indexOf('CropID');
      const idxStageId = h.indexOf('StageID');
      const idxStageName = h.indexOf('StageName');
      const idxSeq = h.indexOf('SequenceNo');
      const idxNFrac = h.indexOf('N_FractionPct');
      const idxPFrac = h.indexOf('P2O5_FractionPct');
      const idxKFrac = h.indexOf('K2O_FractionPct');
      const idxSFrac = h.indexOf('S_FractionPct');

      Logger.log('NutrientStages idxClient=%s idxCrop=%s idxNFrac=%s idxPFrac=%s idxKFrac=%s idxSFrac=%s',
        idxClient, idxCrop, idxNFrac, idxPFrac, idxKFrac, idxSFrac);

      for (let r = 1; r < stData.length; r++) {
        const row = stData[r];
        if (String(row[idxClient]) === String(clientId) &&
          String(row[idxCrop]) === String(cropId)) {
          const stageName = String(row[idxStageName] || row[idxStageId] || '').trim();
          const seq = Number(row[idxSeq] || 0);
          stageRows.push({
            stageName: stageName,
            seq: seq,
            nPct: Number(row[idxNFrac] || 0),
            pPct: Number(row[idxPFrac] || 0),
            kPct: Number(row[idxKFrac] || 0),
            sPct: Number(row[idxSFrac] || 0)
          });
        }
      }

      Logger.log('Matched stageRows: ' + JSON.stringify(stageRows));

      stageRows.sort(function (a, b) { return a.seq - b.seq; });
      stageRows.forEach(function (st) {
        if (!st.stageName) return;
        orderedStages.push(st.stageName);
        if (st.nPct) nFrac[st.stageName] = st.nPct / 100;
        if (st.pPct) pFrac[st.stageName] = st.pPct / 100;
        if (st.kPct) kFrac[st.stageName] = st.kPct / 100;
        if (st.sPct) sFrac[st.stageName] = st.sPct / 100;
      });
    }
  }

  Logger.log('orderedStages after NutrientStages: ' + JSON.stringify(orderedStages));

  // Validate stage fractions sum to 100% (or close to it)
  if (orderedStages.length > 0) {
    const nTotal = Object.values(nFrac).reduce((sum, val) => sum + val, 0);
    const pTotal = Object.values(pFrac).reduce((sum, val) => sum + val, 0);
    const kTotal = Object.values(kFrac).reduce((sum, val) => sum + val, 0);
    
    const tolerance = 0.05;  // 5% tolerance
    if (Math.abs(nTotal - 1.0) > tolerance && nTotal > 0) {
      Logger.log('WARNING: N stage fractions sum to ' + (nTotal * 100).toFixed(1) + '% (expected 100%)');
    }
    if (Math.abs(pTotal - 1.0) > tolerance && pTotal > 0) {
      Logger.log('WARNING: P2O5 stage fractions sum to ' + (pTotal * 100).toFixed(1) + '% (expected 100%)');
    }
    if (Math.abs(kTotal - 1.0) > tolerance && kTotal > 0) {
      Logger.log('WARNING: K2O stage fractions sum to ' + (kTotal * 100).toFixed(1) + '% (expected 100%)');
    }
  }

  if (orderedStages.length === 0) {
    orderedStages.push('Basal', 'Vegetative', 'Flowering');
    Logger.log('No matching NutrientStages rows; using fallback stages.');
  }

  const selectors = buildNutrientSourceSelectors_(clientId);
  Logger.log('selectors keys: ' + Object.keys(selectors || {}));

  const stages = [];
  const nFromPByStage = {};
  const kFromPByStage = {};
  const pFertByStage = {};

  summary.forEach(function (s) {
    let label = s.nutrient;
    let key = s.nutrient;

    if (key === 'P') key = 'P2O5';
    if (key === 'K') key = 'K2O';
    if (label === 'P') label = 'P₂O₅';
    if (label === 'K') label = 'K₂O';

    const base = Number(s.baseDose) || 0;
    const mult = Number(s.doseMultiplier) || 1.0;
    const totalDose = base * mult;

    orderedStages.forEach(function (stageName) {
      let fracMap = null;
      if (key === 'N') fracMap = nFrac;
      else if (key === 'P2O5') fracMap = pFrac;
      else if (key === 'K2O') fracMap = kFrac;
      else if (key === 'S') fracMap = sFrac;

      let frac = 0;
      if (fracMap && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(fracMap, stageName)) {
        frac = fracMap[stageName];
      }

      const dose = frac === 0
        ? 0
        : Math.round(totalDose * frac * 10) / 10;

      if (dose > 0) {
        Logger.log('Dose for %s at %s: base=%s mult=%s frac=%s dose=%s',
          key, stageName, base, mult, frac, dose);
      }

      if (key === 'P2O5' && dose > 0) {
        const stageKey = stageName.toUpperCase();
        let pList = selectors.pByStage[stageKey] || [];

        if (pList.length === 0 && selectors.pByStage.BASAL) {
          pList = selectors.pByStage.BASAL;
        }
        if (pList.length === 0) {
          Object.keys(selectors.pByStage).forEach(function (k) {
            (selectors.pByStage[k] || []).forEach(function (p) { pList.push(p); });
          });
        }

        const src = pList[0] || null;
        if (src && src.P2O5 > 0) {
          const gradeP = src.P2O5;
          const kgProductPerHa = dose / (gradeP / 100);

          const nPercent = Number(src.N || 0);
          const kPercent = Number(src.K2O || 0);

          const nFromThis = kgProductPerHa * (nPercent / 100);
          const kFromThis = kgProductPerHa * (kPercent / 100);

          if (!nFromPByStage[stageName]) nFromPByStage[stageName] = 0;
          if (!kFromPByStage[stageName]) kFromPByStage[stageName] = 0;

          nFromPByStage[stageName] += nFromThis;
          kFromPByStage[stageName] += kFromThis;

          if (!pFertByStage[stageName]) pFertByStage[stageName] = [];
          pFertByStage[stageName].push({
            src: src,
            kgProductPerHa: kgProductPerHa
          });
        }
      }

      stages.push({
        stage: stageName,
        nutrient: label,
        timing: '',
        method: '',
        dose: dose,
        unit: 'kg/ha',
        sourceMix: '',
        productQty: '',
        costPerHa: '',
        details: '',
        flags: ''
      });
    });
  });

  Logger.log('After FIRST PASS, stages.length=' + stages.length);

  const nTargetByStage = {};
  const kTargetByStage = {};
  const sTargetByStage = {};

  summary.forEach(function (s) {
    let key = s.nutrient;
    if (key === 'P') key = 'P2O5';
    if (key === 'K') key = 'K2O';

    const base = Number(s.baseDose) || 0;
    const mult = Number(s.doseMultiplier) || 1.0;
    const totalDose = base * mult;

    orderedStages.forEach(function (stageName) {
      let fracMap = null;
      if (key === 'N') fracMap = nFrac;
      else if (key === 'K2O') fracMap = kFrac;
      else if (key === 'S') fracMap = sFrac;

      if (!fracMap) return;

      const frac = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(fracMap, stageName)
        ? fracMap[stageName]
        : 0;
      if (frac === 0) return;

      const dose = Math.round(totalDose * frac * 10) / 10;

      if (key === 'N') {
        if (!nTargetByStage[stageName]) nTargetByStage[stageName] = 0;
        nTargetByStage[stageName] += dose;
      }
      if (key === 'K2O') {
        if (!kTargetByStage[stageName]) kTargetByStage[stageName] = 0;
        kTargetByStage[stageName] += dose;
      }
      if (key === 'S') {
        if (!sTargetByStage[stageName]) sTargetByStage[stageName] = 0;
        sTargetByStage[stageName] += dose;
      }
    });
  });

  function pickStraightSource(listByStage, stageName, keyField) {
    const stageKey = stageName.toUpperCase();
    let list = (listByStage[stageKey] || []).filter(function (s) {
      return Number(s[keyField] || 0) > 0;
    });
    if (list.length === 0 && listByStage.BASAL) {
      list = (listByStage.BASAL || []).filter(function (s) {
        return Number(s[keyField] || 0) > 0;
      });
    }
    if (list.length === 0) {
      Object.keys(listByStage).forEach(function (k) {
        (listByStage[k] || []).forEach(function (src) {
          if (Number(src[keyField] || 0) > 0) list.push(src);
        });
      });
    }
    return list[0] || null;
  }

  const nByStage = selectors.nByStage || {};
  const kByStage = selectors.kByStage || {};
  const sByStage = selectors.sByStage || {};

  orderedStages.forEach(function (stageName) {
    const pList = pFertByStage[stageName] || [];
    pList.forEach(function (pf) {
      const src = pf.src;
      const kgProductPerHa = pf.kgProductPerHa;
      const unitSize = src.unitSize || 1;
      const unitsPerHa = kgProductPerHa / unitSize;
      const cost = unitsPerHa * (src.pricePerUnit || 0);

      stages.push({
        stage: stageName,
        nutrient: 'P₂O₅',
        timing: '',
        method: '',
        dose: '',
        unit: '',
        sourceMix: src.name,
        productQty: unitsPerHa.toFixed(2) + ' ' + src.unitBasis,
        productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHa * 10) / 10,
        costPerHa: cost.toFixed(0) + ' ' + src.currency,
        details: 'FERT: P complex (DAP or similar)',
        flags: ''
      });
    });

    const nTarget = nTargetByStage[stageName] || 0;
    const nFromP = nFromPByStage[stageName] || 0;
    const nStraight = nTarget - nFromP;
    if (nStraight > 0.05) {
      const nSrc = pickStraightSource(nByStage, stageName, 'N');
      if (nSrc && Number(nSrc.N || 0) > 0) {
        const nGrade = Number(nSrc.N);
        const kgProductPerHaN = nStraight / (nGrade / 100);
        const unitSizeN = nSrc.unitSize || 1;
        const unitsPerHaN = kgProductPerHaN / unitSizeN;
        const costN = unitsPerHaN * (nSrc.pricePerUnit || 0);

        stages.push({
          stage: stageName,
          nutrient: 'N',
          timing: '',
          method: '',
          dose: '',
          unit: '',
          sourceMix: nSrc.name,
          productQty: unitsPerHaN.toFixed(2) + ' ' + nSrc.unitBasis,
          productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHaN * 10) / 10,
          costPerHa: costN.toFixed(0) + ' ' + nSrc.currency,
          details: 'FERT: Straight N (Urea) to close balance after P sources',
          flags: ''
        });
      }
    }

    const kTarget = kTargetByStage[stageName] || 0;
    const kFromP = kFromPByStage[stageName] || 0;
    const kStraight = kTarget - kFromP;
    if (kStraight > 0.05) {
      const kSrc = pickStraightSource(kByStage, stageName, 'K2O');
      if (kSrc && Number(kSrc.K2O || 0) > 0) {
        const kGrade = Number(kSrc.K2O);
        const kgProductPerHaK = kStraight / (kGrade / 100);
        const unitSizeK = kSrc.unitSize || 1;
        const unitsPerHaK = kgProductPerHaK / unitSizeK;
        const costK = unitsPerHaK * (kSrc.pricePerUnit || 0);

        stages.push({
          stage: stageName,
          nutrient: 'K₂O',
          timing: '',
          method: '',
          dose: '',
          unit: '',
          sourceMix: kSrc.name,
          productQty: unitsPerHaK.toFixed(2) + ' ' + kSrc.unitBasis,
          productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHaK * 10) / 10,
          costPerHa: costK.toFixed(0) + ' ' + kSrc.currency,
          details: 'FERT: Straight K (MOP) to close balance after P sources',
          flags: ''
        });
      }
    }

    const sTarget = sTargetByStage[stageName] || 0;
    if (sTarget > 0.05) {
      const sSrc = pickStraightSource(sByStage, stageName, 'S');
      if (sSrc && Number(sSrc.S || 0) > 0) {
        const sGrade = Number(sSrc.S);
        const kgProductPerHaS = sTarget / (sGrade / 100);
        const unitSizeS = sSrc.unitSize || 1;
        const unitsPerHaS = kgProductPerHaS / unitSizeS;
        const costS = unitsPerHaS * (sSrc.pricePerUnit || 0);

        stages.push({
          stage: stageName,
          nutrient: 'S',
          timing: '',
          method: '',
          dose: '',
          unit: '',
          sourceMix: sSrc.name,
          productQty: unitsPerHaS.toFixed(2) + ' ' + sSrc.unitBasis,
          productKgPerHa: Math.round(kgProductPerHaS * 10) / 10,
          costPerHa: costS.toFixed(0) + ' ' + sSrc.currency,
          details: 'FERT: Straight S source',
          flags: ''
        });
      }
    }
  });

  Logger.log('buildGeneralStages_ returning stages.length=' + stages.length);
  return stages;
}


/**
 * Get complete nutrient plan with fertilizer recommendations
 * Public API function that combines nutrient calculation with fertilizer selection
 * 
 * DATA SOURCE: Calls getNutrientPlanForTest() which reads exclusively from SoilTest_Results sheet.
 * 
 * @param {string} testId - Test ID from SoilTest_Results
 * @param {string} deviceId - Device ID (optional)
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme ID (e.g., 'LMH')
 * @param {string} methodId - Method ID (e.g., 'SOIL', 'RDF', 'STCR')
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID
 * @param {number} targetYield - Target yield (required for STCR)
 * @returns {Object} - Complete plan with nutrients and fertilizers
 */
function getNutrientPlanWithFertilizers(testId, deviceId, clientId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, cropId, targetYield) {
  // Get base nutrient plan (reads from SoilTest_Results)
  const nutrientPlan = getNutrientPlanForTest(testId, deviceId, clientId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, cropId, targetYield);
  
  // Check if plan generation failed
  if (nutrientPlan.success === false || !nutrientPlan.stages || nutrientPlan.stages.length === 0) {
    return nutrientPlan;  // Return as-is with error info
  }
  
  // Generate fertilizer recommendations
  const fertilizerPlan = generateFertilizerRecommendation_(nutrientPlan, clientId);
  
  // Combine results
  return {
    success: true,
    testId: testId,
    deviceId: deviceId,
    clientId: clientId,
    cropId: cropId,
    methodId: methodId,
    ratingSchemeId: ratingSchemeId,
    targetYield: targetYield,
    
    // Nutrient requirements
    summary: nutrientPlan.summary,
    stages: nutrientPlan.stages,
    planTotals: nutrientPlan.planTotals,
    
    // Fertilizer recommendations
    fertilizerSchedule: fertilizerPlan.fertilizerSchedule,
    totalCost: fertilizerPlan.totalCost,
    secondaryContributions: fertilizerPlan.secondaryContributions,
    warnings: fertilizerPlan.warnings || []
  };
}


function logNutrientBalance_(testId, stages) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const sheetName = 'NutrientDebug';
  let sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
  if (!sh) {
    sh = ss.insertSheet(sheetName);
    sh.appendRow([
      'Timestamp',
      'TestID',
      'Stage',
      'Nutrient',
      'Target_kg_ha',
      'Supplied_kg_ha',
      'Diff_kg_ha',
      'FertilizerBreakdown'
    ]);
  }

  // Maps: stage -> nutrient -> value
  const target = {};
  const supplied = {};
  const fertText = {};

  stages.forEach(function (row) {
    const stage = row.stage || '';
    const nut = row.nutrient || '';

    if (!stage || !nut) return;

    // Init maps
    if (!target[stage]) target[stage] = {};
    if (!supplied[stage]) supplied[stage] = {};
    if (!fertText[stage]) fertText[stage] = {};

    // Nutrient rows: details empty => target only
    if (!row.details) {
      const dose = Number(row.dose || 0);
      if (!isNaN(dose)) {
        target[stage][nut] = (target[stage][nut] || 0) + dose;
      }
      return;
    }

    // Fertilizer rows: details start with "FERT:"
    if (String(row.details).indexOf('FERT:') !== 0) return;

    const srcName = String(row.sourceMix || '');
    const qtyStr = String(row.productQty || ''); // e.g. "1.74 bag"
    const qtyNum = parseFloat(qtyStr);          // units/ha (bags/ha)
    if (!srcName || isNaN(qtyNum)) return;

    // Look up nutrient analysis and unit size from NutrientSources
    const list = getNutrientSources_('Default'); // or pass clientId later
    const src = (list || []).find(function (s) { return s.name === srcName; });
    if (!src) return;

    const unitSize = src.unitSize || 1; // kg per unit (e.g. per bag)
    const kgPerHa = qtyNum * unitSize;

    const nPct = Number(src.N || 0);
    const pPct = Number(src.P2O5 || 0);
    const kPct = Number(src.K2O || 0);

    function addSupplied(stageKey, nutrientKey, kg) {
      if (!supplied[stageKey][nutrientKey]) supplied[stageKey][nutrientKey] = 0;
      supplied[stageKey][nutrientKey] += kg;
    }

    // Map fertilizer contribution to nutrients
    if (nPct > 0) {
      addSupplied(stage, 'N', kgPerHa * (nPct / 100));
    }
    if (pPct > 0) {
      addSupplied(stage, 'P₂O₅', kgPerHa * (pPct / 100));
    }
    if (kPct > 0) {
      addSupplied(stage, 'K₂O', kgPerHa * (kPct / 100));
    }

    // Track text breakdown per Stage×Nutrient
    ['N', 'P₂O₅', 'K₂O'].forEach(function (nutKey) {
      let contributes = false;
      if (nutKey === 'N' && nPct > 0) contributes = true;
      if (nutKey === 'P₂O₅' && pPct > 0) contributes = true;
      if (nutKey === 'K₂O' && kPct > 0) contributes = true;
      if (!contributes) return;

      if (!fertText[stage][nutKey]) fertText[stage][nutKey] = [];
      fertText[stage][nutKey].push(srcName + ' ' + qtyStr);
    });
  });

  const now = new Date();
  const rowsOut = [];

  Object.keys(target).forEach(function (stage) {
    Object.keys(target[stage]).forEach(function (nut) {
      const tgt = target[stage][nut] || 0;
      const sup = (supplied[stage] && supplied[stage][nut]) ? supplied[stage][nut] : 0;
      const diff = tgt - sup;
      const breakdownArr = (fertText[stage] && fertText[stage][nut]) ? fertText[stage][nut] : [];
      rowsOut.push([
        now,
        testId,
        stage,
        nut,
        Number(tgt.toFixed(2)),
        Number(sup.toFixed(2)),
        Number(diff.toFixed(2)),
        breakdownArr.join(' + ')
      ]);
    });
  });

  if (rowsOut.length > 0) {
    sh.getRange(sh.getLastRow() + 1, 1, rowsOut.length, rowsOut[0].length).setValues(rowsOut);
  }
}

  { k: 'ammonium', t: 'Amnm_n', c: 'Amnm_n_cal', b: 'B' },
  { k: 'phosphorus', t: 'Phprs', c: 'Phprs_cal', b: 'B' },
  { k: 'sulphur', t: 'Slpr', c: 'Slpr_cal', b: 'B' },
  { k: 'oc', t: 'Org_crbn', c: 'Org_crbn_cal', b: 'G' },
  { k: 'nitrate', t: 'Ntrt_n', c: 'Ntrt_n_cal', b: 'G' },
  { k: 'boron', t: 'Brn', c: 'Brn_cal', b: 'B' },
  { k: 'potassium', t: 'Potsm', c: 'Potsm_cal', b: 'B' },
  { k: 'copper', t: 'Cpr', c: 'Cpr_cal', b: 'R' },
