function inventorySheetDefs_() {
  return {
    constitution: {
      name: 'Inventory_Device_Constitution',
      headers: ['Category', 'Item Class', 'Item', 'Unit', 'Units/Device', 'Cost (Rs)/Unit', 'Version', 'Effective Date', 'Cost/Device'],
      fields: ['category', 'itemClass', 'item', 'unit', 'unitsPerDevice', 'costPerUnit', 'version', 'effectiveDate', 'costPerDevice']
    },
    parts: {
      name: 'Inventory_Parts_Stock',
      headers: ['Category', 'Item Class', 'Item', 'Unit', 'In-stock Qty', 'Cost (Rs)/Unit', 'Movement Type', 'Date of Movement', 'Delivery_date', 'PO_number', 'Confirmed by', 'Issued to', 'Request ID'],
      fields: ['category', 'itemClass', 'item', 'unit', 'qty', 'costPerUnit', 'movement', 'date', 'deliveryDate', 'poNumber', 'confirmedBy', 'issuedTo', 'requestId']
    },
    built: {
      name: 'Inventory_Built_Units',
      headers: ['Serial', 'Device_ID', 'Count', 'Date_of_manufacture', 'Date_of_sale', 'Sold_or_issued_to', 'Movement', 'Confirmed', 'Request_ID'],
      fields: ['serial', 'deviceId', 'count', 'mfgDate', 'saleDate', 'soldTo', 'movement', 'confirmed', 'requestId']
    },
    liquidation: {
      name: 'Inventory_Liquidation',
      headers: ['Request_ID', 'Item', 'Issue_or_sale', 'Issued_or_sold_to', 'Units_before', 'Units_after', 'Units_issued', 'Date_issued', 'Issue_acknowledged', 'Evidence_PDF', 'Status'],
      fields: ['requestId', 'item', 'action', 'issuedTo', 'unitsBefore', 'unitsAfter', 'unitsIssued', 'date', 'acknowledged', 'evidence', 'status']
    },
    clients: {
      name: 'Inventory_ClientList',
      headers: ['Client type', 'Client name', 'Client address', 'Client contact', 'Client email', 'Item', 'GSTIN'],
      fields: ['clientType', 'clientName', 'clientAddress', 'clientContact', 'clientEmail', 'item', 'gstin']
    },
    approvals: {
      name: 'Inventory_Approvals',
      headers: ['Request_ID', 'Requested_by', 'TAT_days', 'Due_date', 'Status', 'Approver', 'Item'],
      fields: ['requestId', 'requestedBy', 'tatDays', 'dueDate', 'status', 'approver', 'item']
    },
    refill: {
      name: 'Inventory_Refill_Batch',
      headers: ['Refill Box ID', 'Lot size', 'Refill lot ID', 'Manufacturer name', 'Manufacture date', 'Expiry Date', 'Delivery_date', 'PO_number', 'Notes'],
      fields: ['refillId', 'lotSize', 'refillLotId', 'manufacturerName', 'manufactureDate', 'expiryDate', 'deliveryDate', 'poNumber', 'notes']
    }
  };
}

function ensureInventorySheets_(ss, missingArr) {
  const defs = inventorySheetDefs_();
  Object.values(defs).forEach(def => {
    let sh = ss.getSheetByName(def.name);
    if (!sh) {
      sh = ss.insertSheet(def.name);
      if (Array.isArray(missingArr)) missingArr.push(def.name);
      sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.headers.length).setValues([def.headers]);
      sh.setFrozenRows(1);
    } else {
      const existing = sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, sh.getLastColumn() || def.headers.length).getValues()[0];
      const normalized = existing.map(h => String(h || '').trim());
      const needHeader = def.headers.some((h, i) => normalized[i] !== h);
      if (needHeader) {
        sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.headers.length).setValues([def.headers]);
        sh.setFrozenRows(1);
      }
    }
  });
}

function getInventoryData() {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) throw new Error('No active spreadsheet');
  ensureInventorySheets_(ss);
  const defs = inventorySheetDefs_();

  const parseSheetFloat = (value) => {
    if (value === '' || value === null || value === undefined) return '';
    const num = Number(value);
    if (!Number.isFinite(num)) return value;
    return num.toFixed(2);
  };

  const parseSheetDateTime = (value) => {
    if (!value) return '';
    if (Object.prototype.toString.call(value) === '[object Date]') {
      const year = value.getFullYear();
      const month = String(value.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
      const day = String(value.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
      const hours = String(value.getHours()).padStart(2, '0');
      const minutes = String(value.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0');
      return `${day}-${month}-${year};${hours}:${minutes}`;
    }
    return String(value);
  };

  const readAndMap = (def, mapFn) => {
    const rows = readSheetAsObjects(ss, def.name, []);
    return rows.map(mapFn);
  };

  return {
    constitution: readAndMap(defs.constitution, (r) => ({
      category: r.Category || '',
      itemClass: r['Item Class'] || '',
      item: r.Item || '',
      unit: r.Unit || '',
      unitsPerDevice: parseSheetFloat(r['Units/Device'] || ''),
      costPerUnit: parseSheetFloat(r['Cost (Rs)/Unit'] || ''),
      version: r.Version || '',
      effectiveDate: r['Effective Date'] || '',
      costPerDevice: parseSheetFloat(r['Cost/Device'] || '')
    })),
    parts: readAndMap(defs.parts, (r) => ({
      category: r.Category || '',
      itemClass: r['Item Class'] || '',
      item: r.Item || '',
      unit: r.Unit || '',
      qty: parseSheetFloat(r['In-stock Qty'] || ''),
      costPerUnit: parseSheetFloat(r['Cost (Rs)/Unit'] || ''),
      movement: r['Movement Type'] || '',
      date: parseSheetDateTime(r['Date of Movement'] || ''),
      deliveryDate: parseSheetDateTime(r.Delivery_date || ''),
      poNumber: r.PO_number || '',
      confirmedBy: r['Confirmed by'] || '',
      issuedTo: r['Issued to'] || '',
      requestId: r['Request ID'] || ''
    })),
    built: readAndMap(defs.built, (r) => ({
      serial: r.Serial || '',
      deviceId: r.Device_ID || '',
      count: r.Count || '',
      mfgDate: r.Date_of_manufacture || '',
      saleDate: r.Date_of_sale || '',
      soldTo: r.Sold_or_issued_to || '',
      movement: r.Movement || '',
      confirmed: String(r.Confirmed || '').toLowerCase() === 'true',
      requestId: r.Request_ID || ''
    })),
    liquidation: readAndMap(defs.liquidation, (r) => ({
      requestId: r.Request_ID || '',
      item: r.Item || '',
      action: r.Issue_or_sale || '',
      issuedTo: r.Issued_or_sold_to || '',
      unitsBefore: r.Units_before || '',
      unitsAfter: r.Units_after || '',
      unitsIssued: r.Units_issued || '',
      date: r.Date_issued || '',
      acknowledged: String(r.Issue_acknowledged || '').toLowerCase() === 'true',
      evidence: r.Evidence_PDF || '',
      status: r.Status || ''
    })),
    clients: readAndMap(defs.clients, (r) => ({
      clientType: r['Client type'] || '',
      clientName: r['Client name'] || '',
      clientAddress: r['Client address'] || '',
      clientContact: r['Client contact'] || '',
      clientEmail: r['Client email'] || '',
      item: r.Item || '',
      gstin: r.GSTIN || ''
    })),
    approvals: readAndMap(defs.approvals, (r) => ({
      requestId: r.Request_ID || '',
      requestedBy: r.Requested_by || '',
      tatDays: r.TAT_days || '',
      dueDate: r.Due_date || '',
      status: r.Status || '',
      approver: r.Approver || '',
      item: r.Item || ''
    })),
    refill: readAndMap(defs.refill, (r) => ({
      refillId: r['Refill Box ID'] || '',
      lotSize: parseSheetFloat(r['Lot size'] || ''),
      refillLotId: r['Refill lot ID'] || '',
      manufacturerName: r['Manufacturer name'] || '',
      manufactureDate: parseSheetDateTime(r['Manufacture date'] || ''),
      expiryDate: parseSheetDateTime(r['Expiry Date'] || ''),
      deliveryDate: parseSheetDateTime(r.Delivery_date || ''),
      poNumber: r.PO_number || '',
      notes: r.Notes || ''
    }))
  };
}

function saveInventoryData(payload) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  if (!ss) throw new Error('No active spreadsheet');
  ensureInventorySheets_(ss);
  const defs = inventorySheetDefs_();

  const writeRows = (def, rows) => {
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(def.name);
    if (!sh) return;
    const values = (rows || []).map(row => def.fields.map(f => {
      const v = row?.[f];
      if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'TRUE' : 'FALSE';
      return v ?? '';
    }));
    sh.clearContents();
    sh.getRange(1, 1, 1, def.headers.length).setValues([def.headers]);
    if (values.length) {
      sh.getRange(2, 1, values.length, def.headers.length).setValues(values);
    }
    sh.setFrozenRows(1);
  };

  writeRows(defs.constitution, payload?.constitution);
  writeRows(defs.parts, payload?.parts);
  writeRows(defs.built, payload?.built);
  writeRows(defs.liquidation, payload?.liquidation);
  writeRows(defs.clients, payload?.clients);
  writeRows(defs.approvals, payload?.approvals);
  writeRows(defs.refill, payload?.refill);

  return { ok: true, msg: 'Inventory saved.' };
}

function stvDirect_generatePdfFromHtml(fileName, htmlString) {
  if (!htmlString || !String(htmlString).trim()) {
    throw new Error('stvDirect_generatePdfFromHtml: empty HTML');
  }

  var html = String(htmlString);
  if (!/<!doctype/i.test(html)) {
    html = '<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"></head><body>' + html + '</body></html>';
  }

  var pdfBlob = Utilities.newBlob(html, MimeType.HTML).getAs(MimeType.PDF);
  pdfBlob.setName(fileName || 'STVReport.pdf');

  return {
    ok: true,
    fileName: pdfBlob.getName(),
    mimeType: 'application/pdf',
    pdfBase64: Utilities.base64Encode(pdfBlob.getBytes())
  };
}
/**
 * backend_MN_StateAware
 * 
 * State-aware functions for Nutrient Manager module.
 * Implements State column integration for STCR and RDF calculations.
 * 
 * Key Concepts:
 * - State determines which STCR coefficients apply (e.g., Andhra Pradesh vs Bihar)
 * - Each state has unique yield targets and equations
 * - State="ALL" is used for generic/fallback rows
 * - State matching is case-insensitive with "ALL" fallback
 * 
 * Usage:
 * 1. Extract State from SoilTest_Results using Test_ID
 * 2. Use State to filter config sheets (SoilFertilityRatings, NutrientSTCR, etc.)
 * 3. Fallback to "ALL" if specific state data not found
 */

/**
 * Get State from SoilTest_Results sheet based on Test_ID
 * 
 * @param {string} testId - Test ID to look up
 * @returns {string} State name (e.g., "Andhra Pradesh", "Bihar") or "ALL" as fallback
 */
function getStateFromTest_(deviceId, testId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    
    if (!sh) {
      Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: SoilTest_Results sheet not found, returning ALL');
      return 'ALL';
    }
    
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length < 2) {
      Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: SoilTest_Results sheet is empty');
      return 'ALL';
    }
    
    const headers = data[0];
    
    // Find column indices - State should be at column 3 (index 2)
    // Headers: Place | GPS | State | Test_ID | Device_ID | ...
    const stateIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'STATE');
    const testIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TEST_ID');
    const deviceIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'DEVICE_ID');
    
    if (stateIdx === -1) {
      Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: State column not found in SoilTest_Results, returning ALL');
      return 'ALL';
    }
    
    if (testIdIdx === -1) {
      Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: Test_ID column not found in SoilTest_Results');
      return 'ALL';
    }
    
    if (deviceIdIdx === -1) {
      Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: Device_ID column not found in SoilTest_Results');
      return 'ALL';
    }
    
    // Search for matching Device_ID AND Test_ID pair
    const deviceIdStr = String(deviceId).trim();
    const testIdStr = String(testId).trim();
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const rowDeviceId = String(data[i][deviceIdIdx] || '').trim();
      const rowTestId = String(data[i][testIdIdx] || '').trim();
      if (rowDeviceId === deviceIdStr && rowTestId === testIdStr) {
        const state = String(data[i][stateIdx] || '').trim();
        Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s found State=%s', deviceId, testId, state || 'ALL');
        return state || 'ALL';
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s pair not found, returning ALL', deviceId, testId);
    return 'ALL';
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('getStateFromTest_: Error: %s, returning ALL', e.message);
    return 'ALL';
  }
}

/**
 * Get Place, GPS, and State information for a given Device_ID + Test_ID pair
 * 
 * @param {string} deviceId - Device ID
 * @param {string} testId - Test ID
 * @returns {Object} Object with place, gps, and state properties, or null if not found
 */
function getLocationInfoForTest_(deviceId, testId) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    
    if (!sh) {
      Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: SoilTest_Results sheet not found');
      return null;
    }
    
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length < 2) {
      Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: SoilTest_Results sheet is empty');
      return null;
    }
    
    const headers = data[0];
    
    // Find column indices
    const placeIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'PLACE');
    const gpsIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'GPS');
    const stateIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'STATE');
    const testIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TEST_ID');
    const deviceIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'DEVICE_ID');
    
    if (testIdIdx === -1 || deviceIdIdx === -1) {
      Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: Required columns not found');
      return null;
    }
    
    // Search for matching Device_ID AND Test_ID pair
    const deviceIdStr = String(deviceId).trim();
    const testIdStr = String(testId).trim();
    
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      const rowDeviceId = String(data[i][deviceIdIdx] || '').trim();
      const rowTestId = String(data[i][testIdIdx] || '').trim();
      
      if (rowDeviceId === deviceIdStr && rowTestId === testIdStr) {
        const result = {
          place: placeIdx !== -1 ? String(data[i][placeIdx] || '').trim() : '',
          gps: gpsIdx !== -1 ? String(data[i][gpsIdx] || '').trim() : '',
          state: stateIdx !== -1 ? String(data[i][stateIdx] || '').trim() : '',
          found: true
        };
        Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s found Place=%s GPS=%s State=%s', 
          deviceId, testId, result.place, result.gps, result.state);
        return result;
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s pair not found', deviceId, testId);
    return { place: '', gps: '', state: '', found: false };
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('getLocationInfoForTest_: Error: %s', e.message);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Update Place, GPS, State for a specific RGB_Data row (Device_ID + Test_ID pair)
 * Also propagates to QC_Data and SoilTest_Results sheets if matching rows exist
 */
function updateRGBLocationData_(deviceId, testId, place, gps, state) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const deviceIdStr = String(deviceId || '').trim();
    const testIdStr = String(testId || '').trim();
    
    if (!deviceIdStr || !testIdStr) {
      return { success: false, error: 'Device_ID and Test_ID are required' };
    }

    const placeStr = String(place || '').trim();
    const gpsStr = String(gps || '').trim();
    const stateStr = String(state || '').trim();

    Logger.log('updateRGBLocationData_: Updating Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s Place=%s GPS=%s State=%s',
      deviceId, testId, placeStr, gpsStr, stateStr);

    let updated = 0;

    // 1. Update RGB_Data sheet
    const rgbSheet = ss.getSheetByName('RGB_Data');
    if (rgbSheet) {
      const rgbUpdated = updateSheetLocation_(rgbSheet, deviceIdStr, testIdStr, placeStr, gpsStr, stateStr);
      if (rgbUpdated) updated++;
    }

    // 2. Propagate to QC_Data sheet (if row exists)
    const qcSheet = ss.getSheetByName('QC_Data');
    if (qcSheet) {
      const qcUpdated = updateSheetLocation_(qcSheet, deviceIdStr, testIdStr, placeStr, gpsStr, stateStr);
      if (qcUpdated) updated++;
    }

    // 3. Propagate to SoilTest_Results sheet (if row exists)
    const stSheet = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    if (stSheet) {
      const stUpdated = updateSheetLocation_(stSheet, deviceIdStr, testIdStr, placeStr, gpsStr, stateStr);
      if (stUpdated) updated++;
    }

    Logger.log('updateRGBLocationData_: Updated %d sheet(s)', updated);
    
    return {
      success: true,
      sheetsUpdated: updated,
      message: `Updated location data in ${updated} sheet(s)`
    };

  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('updateRGBLocationData_: Error: %s', e.message);
    return { success: false, error: e.message };
  }
}

/**
 * Helper function to update Place, GPS, State in a specific sheet
 * Returns true if a row was found and updated, false otherwise
 */
function updateSheetLocation_(sheet, deviceId, testId, place, gps, state) {
  if (!sheet) return false;

  const data = sheet.getDataRange().getValues();
  if (data.length < 2) return false;

  const headers = data[0];
  
  // Find column indices
  const deviceIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'DEVICE_ID');
  const testIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TEST_ID');
  const placeIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'PLACE');
  const gpsIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'GPS');
  const stateIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'STATE');

  if (deviceIdIdx === -1 || testIdIdx === -1) {
    Logger.log('updateSheetLocation_: %s missing Device_ID or Test_ID column', sheet.getName());
    return false;
  }

  // Search for matching row
  for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
    const rowDeviceId = String(data[i][deviceIdIdx] || '').trim();
    const rowTestId = String(data[i][testIdIdx] || '').trim();

    if (rowDeviceId === deviceId && rowTestId === testId) {
      // Update Place, GPS, State columns if they exist
      let cellsUpdated = 0;
      
      if (placeIdx !== -1 && place) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, placeIdx + 1).setValue(place);
        cellsUpdated++;
      }
      if (gpsIdx !== -1 && gps) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, gpsIdx + 1).setValue(gps);
        cellsUpdated++;
      }
      if (stateIdx !== -1 && state) {
        sheet.getRange(i + 1, stateIdx + 1).setValue(state);
        cellsUpdated++;
      }

      Logger.log('updateSheetLocation_: %s - Updated row %d (%d cells)', 
        sheet.getName(), i + 1, cellsUpdated);
      return true;
    }
  }

  Logger.log('updateSheetLocation_: %s - No matching row for Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s',
    sheet.getName(), deviceId, testId);
  return false;
}

/**
 * Filter data rows by State with "ALL" fallback
 * 
 * Matching logic:
 * 1. Try exact match (case-insensitive)
 * 2. If no exact match, return rows with State="ALL"
 * 3. If no "ALL" rows, return empty array
 * 
 * @param {Array<Array>} dataRows - Data rows (excluding header)
 * @param {number} stateColIdx - Column index for State
 * @param {string} targetState - State to match (e.g., "Andhra Pradesh")
 * @returns {Array<Array>} Filtered rows matching State or "ALL"
 */
function filterByState_(dataRows, stateColIdx, targetState) {
  if (!dataRows || dataRows.length === 0) {
    return [];
  }
  
  if (stateColIdx === -1 || stateColIdx === undefined || stateColIdx === null) {
    // No State column - return all rows (backward compatibility)
    Logger.log('filterByState_: No State column, returning all rows');
    return dataRows;
  }
  
  const targetStateUpper = String(targetState || 'ALL').trim().toUpperCase();
  
  // First pass: exact match
  const exactMatches = dataRows.filter(row => {
    const rowState = String(row[stateColIdx] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return rowState === targetStateUpper;
  });
  
  if (exactMatches.length > 0) {
    Logger.log('filterByState_: Found %d exact matches for State=%s', exactMatches.length, targetState);
    return exactMatches;
  }
  
  // Second pass: fallback to "ALL"
  const allMatches = dataRows.filter(row => {
    const rowState = String(row[stateColIdx] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    return rowState === 'ALL';
  });
  
  if (allMatches.length > 0) {
    Logger.log('filterByState_: No exact match for State=%s, using %d ALL rows', targetState, allMatches.length);
    return allMatches;
  }
  
  Logger.log('filterByState_: No matches found for State=%s (not even ALL)', targetState);
  return [];
}

/**
 * Get State-aware data from any sheet using headers
 * 
 * This is a State-aware version of getDataByHeaders that automatically
 * filters by State column if present.
 * 
 * @param {Spreadsheet} ss - Spreadsheet object
 * @param {string} sheetName - Sheet name
 * @param {Array<string>} headerNames - Column headers to retrieve (should include 'State')
 * @param {string} targetState - State to filter by (optional, defaults to 'ALL')
 * @returns {Array<Array>} Filtered data rows matching State
 */
function getDataByHeadersWithState_(ss, sheetName, headerNames, targetState) {
  try {
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName(sheetName);
    if (!sh) {
      Logger.log('getDataByHeadersWithState_: Sheet %s not found', sheetName);
      return [];
    }
    
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length < 1) {
      return [];
    }
    
    const headers = data[0];
    const rows = data.slice(1);
    
    // Build column index map
    const colMap = {};
    headerNames.forEach(hn => {
      const idx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === String(hn).trim().toUpperCase());
      if (idx !== -1) {
        colMap[hn] = idx;
      }
    });
    
    // Find State column index
    const stateIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'STATE');
    
    // Filter by State if State column exists
    let filteredRows = rows;
    if (stateIdx !== -1 && targetState) {
      filteredRows = filterByState_(rows, stateIdx, targetState);
    }
    
    // Extract requested columns from filtered rows
    const result = filteredRows.map(row => {
      return headerNames.map(hn => {
        const idx = colMap[hn];
        return idx !== undefined ? row[idx] : '';
      });
    });
    
    Logger.log('getDataByHeadersWithState_: Sheet=%s State=%s returned %d rows', 
      sheetName, targetState, result.length);
    
    return result;
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('getDataByHeadersWithState_: Error: %s', e.message);
    return [];
  }
}

/**
 * Get base dose from NutrientTargets with State filtering
 * 
 * Enhanced version of getBaseDoseFromTargets_() that includes State parameter.
 * 
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID (e.g., "WHEAT", "Wheat")
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme (e.g., "LMH")
 * @param {string} methodId - Method ID (e.g., "SOIL", "STCR")
 * @param {string} nutrientParam - Nutrient parameter (N, P2O5, K2O, etc.)
 * @param {string} state - State name (e.g., "Andhra Pradesh") or "ALL"
 * @returns {Object} {baseDose, dosePerTonneYield, maxDose, minDose} or null
 */
function getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_(clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, nutrientParam, state) {
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    
    // NutrientTargets columns: State | ClientID | CropID | RatingSchemeID | MethodID | Parameter | BaseDosePerHa | DosePerTonneYield | MaxDosePerHa | MinDosePerHa
    const data = getDataByHeadersWithState_(ss, 'NutrientTargets', [
      'State', 'ClientID', 'CropID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'MethodID', 'Parameter',
      'BaseDosePerHa', 'DosePerTonneYield', 'MaxDosePerHa', 'MinDosePerHa'
    ], state || 'ALL');
    
    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
      Logger.log('getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_: No data found for State=%s', state);
      return null;
    }
    
    const cropUpper = String(cropId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    
    // Try exact match with all parameters
    for (const row of data) {
      const rowClient = String(row[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowCrop = String(row[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowRating = String(row[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowMethod = String(row[4] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowParam = String(row[5] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      
      if (rowClient === String(clientId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
          rowCrop === cropUpper &&
          rowRating === String(ratingSchemeId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
          rowMethod === String(methodId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
          rowParam === String(nutrientParam || '').trim().toUpperCase()) {
        
        Logger.log('getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_: Found exact match for State=%s Crop=%s Parameter=%s',
          state, cropId, nutrientParam);
        
        return {
          baseDose: Number(row[6]) || 0,
          dosePerTonneYield: Number(row[7]) || 0,
          maxDose: Number(row[8]) || 999,
          minDose: Number(row[9]) || 0
        };
      }
    }
    
    Logger.log('getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_: No exact match found for State=%s Crop=%s Parameter=%s',
      state, cropId, nutrientParam);
    return null;
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_: Error: %s', e.message);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Compute STCR dose with State filtering
 * 
 * Enhanced version of computeDoseFromSTCR_() that includes State parameter.
 * Reads state-specific STCR coefficients from NutrientSTCR sheet.
 * 
 * @param {string} state - State name (e.g., "Andhra Pradesh")
 * @param {string} clientID - Client ID
 * @param {string} cropID - Crop ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeID - Rating scheme ID
 * @param {string} nutrient - Nutrient name (N, P2O5, K2O)
 * @param {number} targetYield - Target yield in t/ha
 * @param {number} soilTestValue - Soil test value in kg/ha
 * @param {number} organicIndex - Organic contribution in kg/ha
 * @param {string} methodID - Method ID (optional)
 * @returns {Object} {dose_kg_ha, minDose, maxDose} or null
 */
function computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_(state, clientID, cropID, ratingSchemeID, nutrient, targetYield, soilTestValue, organicIndex, methodID) {
  Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_ called: State=%s clientID=%s cropID=%s nutrient=%s targetYield=%s soilTest=%s organicIndex=%s',
    state, clientID, cropID, nutrient, targetYield, soilTestValue, organicIndex);
  
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    
    // NutrientSTCR columns: State | ClientID | CropID | RatingSchemeID | Nutrient | EquationType | CoeffYield | CoeffSoil | CoeffOrganic | MinDosePerHa | MaxDosePerHa
    const data = getDataByHeadersWithState_(ss, 'NutrientSTCR', [
      'State', 'ClientID', 'CropID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'Nutrient', 'EquationType',
      'CoeffYield', 'CoeffSoil', 'CoeffOrganic', 'MinDosePerHa', 'MaxDosePerHa', 'MethodID'
    ], state || 'ALL');
    
    if (!data || data.length === 0) {
      Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_: No STCR data found for State=%s', state);
      return null;
    }
    
    const cropUpper = String(cropID || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    
    // Find matching row
    let matchRow = null;
    for (const row of data) {
      const rowClient = String(row[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowCrop = String(row[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowRating = String(row[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowNutrient = String(row[4] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      
      if (rowClient === String(clientID || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
          rowCrop === cropUpper &&
          rowRating === String(ratingSchemeID || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
          rowNutrient === String(nutrient || '').trim().toUpperCase()) {
        
        matchRow = row;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (!matchRow) {
      Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_: No matching STCR equation for State=%s Crop=%s Nutrient=%s',
        state, cropID, nutrient);
      return null;
    }
    
    // Extract coefficients
    const coeffYield = Number(matchRow[6]) || 0;
    const coeffSoil = Number(matchRow[7]) || 0;
    const coeffOrganic = Number(matchRow[8]) || 0;
    const minDose = Number(matchRow[9]) || 0;
    const maxDose = Number(matchRow[10]) || 999;
    
    // Calculate dose using STCR formula: F = Cy*Y - Cs*S - Co*O
    const dose = (coeffYield * targetYield) - (coeffSoil * soilTestValue) - (coeffOrganic * organicIndex);
    
    Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_: State=%s STCR equation: dose = %s*%s - %s*%s - %s*%s = %s',
      state, coeffYield, targetYield, coeffSoil, soilTestValue, coeffOrganic, organicIndex, dose);
    
    // Apply min/max constraints
    const finalDose = Math.max(minDose, Math.min(maxDose, dose));
    
    Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_: State=%s Final dose=%s (after min=%s max=%s constraints)',
      state, finalDose, minDose, maxDose);
    
    return {
      dose_kg_ha: finalDose,
      minDose: minDose,
      maxDose: maxDose,
      coefficients: {
        coeffYield: coeffYield,
        coeffSoil: coeffSoil,
        coeffOrganic: coeffOrganic
      }
    };
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_: Error: %s', e.message);
    return null;
  }
}

/**
 * Test function to verify State integration
 * 
 * Run this to test State-aware functions with sample data.
 * Assumes sheets have been populated with State column and test data.
 */
function testStateIntegration_() {
  Logger.log('=== Testing State Integration ===');
  
  // Test 1: Get State from Device_ID + Test_ID pair
  const deviceId = '42335';  // Sample device ID
  const testId = '41441';
  const state = getStateFromTest_(deviceId, testId);
  Logger.log('Test 1: getStateFromTest_(deviceId=%s, testId=%s) = %s', deviceId, testId, state);
  
  // Test 2: Test filter by state
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  const testData = [
    ['Andhra Pradesh', 'Default', 'WHEAT', 'N', 100],
    ['Bihar', 'Default', 'WHEAT', 'N', 80],
    ['ALL', 'Default', 'WHEAT', 'N', 90]
  ];
  
  const filtered1 = filterByState_(testData, 0, 'Andhra Pradesh');
  Logger.log('Test 2a: filterByState_(Andhra Pradesh) returned %d rows', filtered1.length);
  
  const filtered2 = filterByState_(testData, 0, 'Tamil Nadu');
  Logger.log('Test 2b: filterByState_(Tamil Nadu - not in data) returned %d rows (should be ALL)', filtered2.length);
  
  // Test 3: Get base dose with State
  const baseDose = getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_('Default', 'WHEAT', 'LMH', 'SOIL', 'N', state);
  Logger.log('Test 3: getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_ for State=%s returned: %s', state, JSON.stringify(baseDose));
  
  // Test 4: Compute STCR with State
  const stcrResult = computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_(state, 'Default', 'WHEAT', 'LMH', 'N', 5, 220, 6.3, 'STCR');
  Logger.log('Test 4: computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_ for State=%s returned: %s', state, JSON.stringify(stcrResult));
  
  Logger.log('=== State Integration Tests Complete ===');
}
/**
 * backend_MN_StateIntegration
 * 
 * Integration layer that connects State-aware functions to main Nutrient Manager flow.
 * This file bridges backend_MN_StateAware functions with existing backend_MN_v1 functions.
 * 
 * Main Functions:
 * - getNutrientPlanForTestWithState() - State-aware version of getNutrientPlanForTest()
 * - Wraps existing functions with State parameter extraction and filtering
 * 
 * Usage Flow:
 * 1. Extract Test_ID from UI
 * 2. Get State from SoilTest_Results using Test_ID
 * 3. Pass State to all config sheet queries
 * 4. Return state-specific nutrient recommendations
 */

/**
 * Get nutrient plan with State integration
 * 
 * This is a State-aware wrapper around getNutrientPlanForTest().
 * Automatically extracts State from SoilTest_Results and uses it for all queries.
 * 
 * @param {string} testId - Test ID from SoilTest_Results
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme (e.g., "LMH")
 * @param {string} methodId - Method ID (e.g., "STCR", "RDF")
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID (e.g., "WHEAT")
 * @param {number} targetYield - Target yield for STCR (optional)
 * @returns {Array} Nutrient plan stages or error object
 */
function getNutrientPlanForTestWithState(testId, deviceId, clientId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, cropId, targetYield) {
  Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState called: testId=%s deviceId=%s clientId=%s methodId=%s cropId=%s targetYield=%s',
    testId, deviceId, clientId, methodId, cropId, targetYield);
  
  try {
    // Step 1: Get State from Device_ID + Test_ID pair
    const state = getStateFromTest_(deviceId, testId);
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Extracted State=%s from Device_ID=%s Test_ID=%s', state, deviceId, testId);
    
    // Step 2: Read soil test data from SoilTest_Results (unchanged - already reads from SoilTest_Results)
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    
    if (!sh) {
      Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: SoilTest_Results sheet not found');
      return { success: false, errors: ['SoilTest_Results sheet not found'] };
    }
    
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    if (data.length < 2) {
      return { success: false, errors: ['SoilTest_Results sheet is empty'] };
    }
    
    const headers = data[0];
    const testIdIdx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === 'TEST_ID');
    
    if (testIdIdx === -1) {
      return { success: false, errors: ['Test_ID column not found in SoilTest_Results'] };
    }
    
    // Find test row
    let testRow = null;
    for (let i = 1; i < data.length; i++) {
      if (String(data[i][testIdIdx]).trim() === String(testId).trim()) {
        testRow = data[i];
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (!testRow) {
      Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Test_ID=%s not found', testId);
      return { success: false, errors: ['Test_ID not found: ' + testId] };
    }
    
    // Extract soil test values
    const getColValue = (colName) => {
      const idx = headers.findIndex(h => String(h).trim().toUpperCase() === colName.toUpperCase());
      return idx !== -1 ? testRow[idx] : null;
    };
    
    const soilData = {
      pH: Number(getColValue('pH')) || 0,
      EC: Number(getColValue('EC')) || 0,
      OC: Number(getColValue('OC')) || 0,
      Avail_N: Number(getColValue('Avail_N')) || 0,
      Avail_P: Number(getColValue('Avail_P')) || 0,
      Avail_K: Number(getColValue('Avail_K')) || 0,
      Avail_S: Number(getColValue('Avail_S')) || 0,
      Zn: Number(getColValue('Zn')) || 0,
      Fe: Number(getColValue('Fe')) || 0,
      Mn: Number(getColValue('Mn')) || 0,
      Cu: Number(getColValue('Cu')) || 0,
      B: Number(getColValue('B')) || 0
    };
    
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Soil test data: OC=%s Avail_N=%s Avail_P=%s Avail_K=%s',
      soilData.OC, soilData.Avail_N, soilData.Avail_P, soilData.Avail_K);
    
    // Step 3: Calculate organic nutrient contribution
    const organicContrib = calculateOrganicNutrientContribution_(soilData.OC);
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Organic contribution: N=%s P2O5=%s K2O=%s',
      organicContrib.N, organicContrib.P2O5, organicContrib.K2O);
    
    // Step 4: Get State-aware soil fertility ratings
    const summary = getSoilFertilityRatingsWithState_(state, clientId, ratingSchemeId, soilData);
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Got %d nutrient ratings for State=%s', summary.length, state);
    
    // Step 5: Determine if using STCR or RDF
    const useSTCR = methodId && (String(methodId).toUpperCase().indexOf('STCR') >= 0) && 
                    targetYield && Number(targetYield) > 0;
    
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Mode detection: methodId=%s useSTCR=%s targetYield=%s',
      methodId, useSTCR, targetYield);
    
    // Step 6: Build stages with State-aware calculations
    let stages = [];
    if (useSTCR) {
      Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Using STCR mode with State=%s targetYield=%s', state, targetYield);
      stages = buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, summary, targetYield, organicContrib);
    } else {
      Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Using RDF mode with State=%s', state);
      stages = buildStagesWithRDFAndState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, summary);
    }
    
    // Step 7: Add metadata
    if (stages && stages.length > 0) {
      stages.forEach(stage => {
        stage.state = state;
        stage.testId = testId;
      });
    }
    
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Returning %d stages for State=%s', stages.length, state);
    return stages;
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('getNutrientPlanForTestWithState: Error: %s', e.message);
    return { success: false, errors: [e.message] };
  }
}

/**
 * Get soil fertility ratings with State filtering
 * 
 * @param {string} state - State name
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme ID
 * @param {Object} soilData - Soil test values
 * @returns {Array} Summary array with nutrient ratings
 */
function getSoilFertilityRatingsWithState_(state, clientId, ratingSchemeId, soilData) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  
  // SoilFertilityRatings columns: State | ClientID | RatingSchemeID | Parameter | ClassName | ClassOrder | MinValue | MaxValue | Unit | Notes | DoseMultiplier | BaseDose_kg_ha
  const fert = getDataByHeadersWithState_(ss, 'SoilFertilityRatings', [
    'State', 'ClientID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'Parameter', 'ClassName', 'ClassOrder',
    'MinValue', 'MaxValue', 'Unit', 'Notes', 'DoseMultiplier'
  ], state);
  
  Logger.log('getSoilFertilityRatingsWithState_: Loaded %d rows for State=%s', fert.length, state);
  
  const summary = [];
  const paramMapping = {
    'Avail_N': 'N',
    'Avail_P': 'P2O5',
    'Avail_K': 'K2O',
    'Avail_S': 'S',
    'Zn': 'Zn',
    'Fe': 'Fe',
    'Mn': 'Mn',
    'Cu': 'Cu',
    'B': 'B'
  };
  
  for (const [soilKey, paramName] of Object.entries(paramMapping)) {
    const soilVal = soilData[soilKey];
    if (soilVal === undefined || soilVal === null) continue;
    
    // Filter ratings for this parameter (State already filtered by getDataByHeadersWithState_)
    const matches = fert.filter(r => {
      const rowClient = String(r[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowRating = String(r[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      const rowParam = String(r[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
      
      return rowClient === String(clientId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
             rowRating === String(ratingSchemeId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
             rowParam === paramName.toUpperCase();
    });
    
    let className = '', unit = '', notes = '', doseMultiplier = 1;
    matches.forEach(r => {
      const min = r[6], max = r[7];
      if ((min === '' || soilVal >= min) && (max === '' || soilVal <= max)) {
        className = r[4];
        unit = r[8];
        notes = r[9];
        doseMultiplier = Number(r[10]) || 1;
      }
    });
    
    // Get base dose from NutrientTargets (State-aware)
    const targetInfo = getBaseDoseFromTargetsWithState_(clientId, 'ALL', ratingSchemeId, 'SOIL', paramName, state);
    const baseDose = targetInfo ? targetInfo.baseDose : getDefaultBaseDose_(paramName);
    
    Logger.log('getSoilFertilityRatingsWithState_: State=%s Nutrient=%s soilVal=%s className=%s doseMultiplier=%s baseDose=%s',
      state, paramName, soilVal, className, doseMultiplier, baseDose);
    
    summary.push({
      nutrient: paramName,
      soilValue: soilVal,
      unit: unit || '-',
      className: className || '-',
      notes: notes || '',
      baseDose: baseDose,
      doseMultiplier: doseMultiplier
    });
  }
  
  return summary;
}

/**
 * Build stages using STCR with State integration
 * 
 * @param {string} state - State name
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme ID
 * @param {string} methodId - Method ID
 * @param {Array} summary - Soil test summary
 * @param {number} targetYield - Target yield
 * @param {Object} organicContrib - Organic nutrient contribution
 * @returns {Array} Stages with STCR doses
 */
function buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, summary, targetYield, organicContrib) {
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: State=%s cropId=%s targetYield=%s', state, cropId, targetYield);
  
  // Prepare soil values and organic index by parameter
  const soilValuesByParam = {};
  const organicIndexByParam = {};
  
  summary.forEach(s => {
    soilValuesByParam[s.nutrient] = s.soilValue;
  });
  
  organicIndexByParam['N'] = organicContrib.N || 0;
  organicIndexByParam['P2O5'] = organicContrib.P2O5 || 0;
  organicIndexByParam['K2O'] = organicContrib.K2O || 0;
  
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: soilValues=%s organicIndex=%s',
    JSON.stringify(soilValuesByParam), JSON.stringify(organicIndexByParam));
  
  // Get ordered stages
  const orderedStages = getOrderedStagesWithState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId);
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: Found %d stages for State=%s', orderedStages.length, state);
  
  // Calculate total dose for each nutrient using STCR
  const totalDosesByNutrient = {};
  
  for (const s of summary) {
    const nutrient = s.nutrient;
    const soilTest = soilValuesByParam[nutrient] || 0;
    const organicIdx = organicIndexByParam[nutrient] || 0;
    
    // Call State-aware STCR function
    const stcrResult = computeDoseFromSTCRWithState_(
      state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, nutrient,
      targetYield, soilTest, organicIdx, methodId
    );
    
    if (stcrResult && stcrResult.dose_kg_ha !== undefined) {
      totalDosesByNutrient[nutrient] = stcrResult.dose_kg_ha;
      Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: State=%s Nutrient=%s STCR dose=%s kg/ha',
        state, nutrient, stcrResult.dose_kg_ha);
    } else {
      // Fallback to RDF if STCR not available for this nutrient
      totalDosesByNutrient[nutrient] = s.baseDose * s.doseMultiplier;
      Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: State=%s Nutrient=%s using RDF fallback=%s kg/ha',
        state, nutrient, totalDosesByNutrient[nutrient]);
    }
  }
  
  // Distribute total doses across stages
  const stages = [];
  
  for (const stg of orderedStages) {
    const stageObj = {
      stageName: stg.stageName,
      sequence: stg.seq,
      nutrients: []
    };
    
    for (const [nutrient, totalDose] of Object.entries(totalDosesByNutrient)) {
      const fraction = stg.fractions[nutrient] || 0;
      const stageDose = totalDose * fraction;
      
      stageObj.nutrients.push({
        nutrient: nutrient,
        dose_kg_ha: stageDose,
        fraction: fraction,
        totalDose: totalDose
      });
    }
    
    stages.push(stageObj);
  }
  
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithSTCRAndState_: Returning %d stages for State=%s', stages.length, state);
  return stages;
}

/**
 * Build stages using RDF with State integration
 * 
 * @param {string} state - State name
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme ID
 * @param {string} methodId - Method ID
 * @param {Array} summary - Soil test summary
 * @returns {Array} Stages with RDF doses
 */
function buildStagesWithRDFAndState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId, methodId, summary) {
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithRDFAndState_: State=%s cropId=%s', state, cropId);
  
  // Get ordered stages
  const orderedStages = getOrderedStagesWithState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId);
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithRDFAndState_: Found %d stages for State=%s', orderedStages.length, state);
  
  const stages = [];
  
  for (const stg of orderedStages) {
    const stageObj = {
      stageName: stg.stageName,
      sequence: stg.seq,
      nutrients: []
    };
    
    for (const s of summary) {
      const nutrient = s.nutrient;
      const baseDose = s.baseDose || 0;
      const multiplier = s.doseMultiplier || 1;
      const fraction = stg.fractions[nutrient] || 0;
      
      const totalDose = baseDose * multiplier;
      const stageDose = totalDose * fraction;
      
      stageObj.nutrients.push({
        nutrient: nutrient,
        dose_kg_ha: stageDose,
        fraction: fraction,
        baseDose: baseDose,
        multiplier: multiplier,
        className: s.className
      });
    }
    
    stages.push(stageObj);
  }
  
  Logger.log('buildStagesWithRDFAndState_: Returning %d stages for State=%s', stages.length, state);
  return stages;
}

/**
 * Get ordered stages with State filtering
 * 
 * @param {string} state - State name
 * @param {string} clientId - Client ID
 * @param {string} cropId - Crop ID
 * @param {string} ratingSchemeId - Rating scheme ID
 * @returns {Array} Ordered stages with fractions
 */
function getOrderedStagesWithState_(state, clientId, cropId, ratingSchemeId) {
  const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
  
  // NutrientStages columns: State | ClientID | CropID | RatingSchemeID | Stage | Seq | N_Fraction | P2O5_Fraction | K2O_Fraction | S_Fraction | ...
  const data = getDataByHeadersWithState_(ss, 'NutrientStages', [
    'State', 'ClientID', 'CropID', 'RatingSchemeID', 'Stage', 'Seq',
    'N_Fraction', 'P2O5_Fraction', 'K2O_Fraction', 'S_Fraction'
  ], state);
  
  Logger.log('getOrderedStagesWithState_: Loaded %d stage rows for State=%s', data.length, state);
  
  const cropUpper = String(cropId || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const stages = [];
  
  for (const row of data) {
    const rowClient = String(row[1] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const rowCrop = String(row[2] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    const rowRating = String(row[3] || '').trim().toUpperCase();
    
    if (rowClient === String(clientId || '').trim().toUpperCase() &&
        rowCrop === cropUpper &&
        rowRating === String(ratingSchemeId || '').trim().toUpperCase()) {
      
      stages.push({
        stageName: row[4],
        seq: Number(row[5]) || 0,
        fractions: {
          'N': Number(row[6]) || 0,
          'P2O5': Number(row[7]) || 0,
          'K2O': Number(row[8]) || 0,
          'S': Number(row[9]) || 0
        }
      });
    }
  }
  
  // Sort by sequence
  stages.sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq);
  
  // Fallback to single Basal stage if no stages found
  if (stages.length === 0) {
    Logger.log('getOrderedStagesWithState_: No stages found for State=%s, using default Basal', state);
    stages.push({
      stageName: 'Basal',
      seq: 1,
      fractions: { 'N': 1, 'P2O5': 1, 'K2O': 1, 'S': 1 }
    });
  }
  
  return stages;
}

/**
 * Get default base dose (fallback)
 */
function getDefaultBaseDose_(nutrient) {
  const defaults = {
    'N': 120,
    'P2O5': 60,
    'K2O': 120,
    'S': 10,
    'Zn': 5,
    'Fe': 5,
    'Mn': 5,
    'Cu': 2,
    'B': 1
  };
  return defaults[nutrient] || 0;
}

function testStateIntegrationEndToEnd_() {
  Logger.log('=== Testing State Integration End-to-End ===');
  
  // Test with actual Device_ID and Test_ID

    'WHEAT',

  Logger.log('Test result: %s', JSON.stringify(result, null, 2));
  
  Logger.log('=== Test Complete ===');
}

// ------------------------------
// DIAGNOSTIC FUNCTION: Debug SoilTest_Results Reading
// ------------------------------
/**
 * Diagnostic function to help debug SoilTest_Results reading issues.
 * Run this from Apps Script editor to see what's happening with sheet reading.
 * 
 * Usage:
 * 1. Open Apps Script editor
 * 2. Select diagnoseSoilTestResults_() from function dropdown
 * 3. Edit the testId variable below to use your actual Test_ID
 * 4. Click Run ▶️
 * 5. View logs (Ctrl+Enter or View → Logs)
 */
function diagnoseSoilTestResults_() {
  Logger.log('=== DIAGNOSTIC: SoilTest_Results Reading ===');
  
  // EDIT THIS: Put your actual Test_ID here
  const testId = '41441';  // Change this to your Test_ID
  
  Logger.log('Looking for Test_ID: ' + testId);
  
  try {
    const ss = SpreadsheetApp.getActiveSpreadsheet();
    const sh = ss.getSheetByName('SoilTest_Results');
    
    if (!sh) {
      Logger.log('ERROR: SoilTest_Results sheet not found!');
      return;
    }
    
    Logger.log('✓ Sheet found: SoilTest_Results');
    
    const data = sh.getDataRange().getValues();
    Logger.log('Sheet has ' + (data.length - 1) + ' data rows (plus 1 header row)');
    
    if (data.length < 2) {
      Logger.log('ERROR: Sheet has no data rows!');
      return;
    }
    
    // Show headers
    const headers = data[0].map(String);
    Logger.log('Headers (' + headers.length + ' columns): ' + JSON.stringify(headers));
    
    // Find Test_ID column
    const idxTestId = getHeaderIndex_(headers, 'Test_ID');
    Logger.log('Test_ID column index: ' + idxTestId);
    
    if (idxTestId === -1) {
      Logger.log('ERROR: Test_ID column not found in headers!');
      Logger.log('Available headers: ' + headers.join(', '));
      return;
    }
    
    // Search for Test_ID
    let found = false;
    for (let r = 1; r < data.length; r++) {
      const rowTestId = String(data[r][idxTestId]);
      if (rowTestId === String(testId)) {
        Logger.log('✓ Found Test_ID at row ' + (r + 1));
        
        // Build row object
        const rowObj = {};
        headers.forEach((h, i) => rowObj[h] = data[r][i]);
        
        Logger.log('Row data: ' + JSON.stringify(rowObj, null, 2));
        
        // Check soil parameters
        const params = ['pH', 'EC', 'OC', 'Avail_N', 'Avail_P', 'Avail_K', 'Avail_S', 'Zn', 'Fe', 'Mn', 'Cu', 'B'];
        Logger.log('Soil parameter indices:');
        params.forEach(p => {
          const idx = getHeaderIndex_(headers, p);
          const val = idx >= 0 ? rowObj[p] : 'NOT FOUND';
          Logger.log('  ' + p + ' (index ' + idx + '): ' + val);
        });
        
        found = true;
        break;
      }
    }
    
    if (!found) {
      Logger.log('ERROR: Test_ID ' + testId + ' not found in sheet!');
      Logger.log('Available Test_IDs (first 10):');
      for (let r = 1; r < Math.min(11, data.length); r++) {
        Logger.log('  Row ' + (r + 1) + ': ' + String(data[r][idxTestId]));
      }
    }
    
  } catch (e) {
    Logger.log('ERROR: ' + e.toString());
    Logger.log('Stack: ' + e.stack);
  }
  
  Logger.log('=== Diagnostic Complete ===');
}
